`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/core/telemetry/PipelineTracker.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.core.telemetry

import android.content.Context
import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.core.net.toUri
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants.AUTHORITY
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants.METHOD_NOTIFY_CAPTURE
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants.METHOD_RECORD_NODE
import java.util.Collections
import java.util.concurrent.Executors

/**
 * Tracks the hook pipeline nodes executed by the target application process
 * and reports telemetry / capture notifications to xCam's SettingsProvider.
 */
object PipelineTracker {
    private val activeNodes = Collections.synchronizedSet(LinkedHashSet<String>())
    private val executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()

    fun reset() {
        activeNodes.clear()
    }

    fun recordNode(context: Context?, node: String) {
        if (activeNodes.add(node)) {
            val ctx = context ?: return
            executor.execute {
                try {
                    val extras = Bundle().apply {
                        putString("node", node)
                        putString("route", getRouteSummary())
                    }
                    ctx.contentResolver.call(
                        "content://$AUTHORITY".toUri(),
                        METHOD_RECORD_NODE,
                        ctx.packageName,
                        extras
                    )
                } catch (_: Throwable) {}
            }
        }
    }

    fun getRouteSummary(): String {
        synchronized(activeNodes) {
            return if (activeNodes.isEmpty()) "Default" else activeNodes.joinToString(" ➔ ")
        }
    }

    fun reportCapture(context: Context?, width: Int, height: Int, thumbnail: ByteArray? = null) {
        val ctx = context ?: return
        val currentRoute = getRouteSummary()
        executor.execute {
            try {
                val extras = Bundle().apply {
                    putString("route", currentRoute)
                    putInt("width", width)
                    putInt("height", height)
                    if (thumbnail != null && thumbnail.size <= 256 * 1024) {
                        putByteArray("thumbnail", thumbnail)
                    }
                }
                ctx.contentResolver.call(
                    "content://$AUTHORITY".toUri(),
                    METHOD_NOTIFY_CAPTURE,
                    ctx.packageName,
                    extras
                )
            } catch (_: Throwable) {}
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/core/virtualcamera/EglFrameRenderer.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.core.virtualcamera

import android.graphics.ImageFormat
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.graphics.YuvImage
import android.media.Image
import android.media.Image.Plane
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import android.view.Surface
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import java.nio.FloatBuffer
import javax.microedition.khronos.egl
import javax.microedition.khronos.opengles.GL10
import javax.microedition.khronos.opengles.GL20

/**
 * OpenGL ES / EGL frame renderer for the virtual camera.
 * Converts YUV_420_888 or NV21 frames to output surfaces (preview or JPEG).
 *
 * Pipeline:
 * - Input: YUV_420_888 frames from MediaCodecYuvDecoder or NV21 from YuvFrameProcessor
 * - Processing: EGL texture with YUV->RGB shader
 * - Output: NV21 bytes (for preview) or JPEG bytes (for still capture)
 */
class EglFrameRenderer(private val logAction: (String) -> Unit) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xEglFrame"
        private const val EGL_CONTEXT_CLIENT_VERSION = 0x3098
        private const val EGL_OPENGL_ES2_BIT = 4
        private const val EGL_DEFAULT_DISPLAY = 0x00000000
        private const val EGL_RED_SIZE = 0x3024
        private const val EGL_GREEN_SIZE = 0x3025
        private const val EGL_BLUE_SIZE = 0x3026
        private const val EGL_ALPHA_SIZE = 0x3027
        private const val EGL_RENDERABLE_TYPE = 0x3040
        private const val EGL_NONE = 0x3038
        private const val EGL_SUCCESS = 12297
        private const val EGL_FALSE = 0
        private const val EGL_TRUE = 1
        private const val EGL_NO_CONTEXT = 0
        private const val EGL_NO_DISPLAY = 0
        private const val EGL_NO_SURFACE = 0

        // Fragment shader for YUV_420_888 -> RGB conversion
        private const val YUV_FRAGMENT_SHADER = """
            #extension GL_OES_EGL_image_external : require
            precision mediump float;
            varying vec2 vTextureCoord;
            uniform samplerExternal2D yTexture;
            uniform samplerExternal2D uTexture;
            uniform samplerExternal2D vTexture;
            void main() {
                float r, g, b, y, u, v;
                y = texture2D(yTexture, vTextureCoord).r;
                u = texture2D(uTexture, vTextureCoord).r - 0.5;
                v = texture2D(vTexture, vTextureCoord).r - 0.5;
                r = y + 1.402 * v;
                g = y - 0.344136 * u - 0.714136 * v;
                b = y + 1.771 * u;
                gl_FragColor = vec4(r, g, b, 1.0);
            }
        """.trimIndent()

        // Vertex shader (standard quad)
        private const val YUV_VERTEX_SHADER = """
            attribute vec4 vPosition;
            attribute vec2 vTextureCoord;
            varying vec2 vTextureCoord;
            void main() {
                vTextureCoord = vTextureCoord;
                gl_Position = vPosition;
            }
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    private var glInitialized = false
    private var glContext: EGLContext? = null
    private var glDisplay: EGLDisplay? = null
    private var glSurface: EGLSurface? = null
    private var glProgram: Int = -1
    private var vPositionHandle: Int = -1
    private var vTextureCoordHandle: Int = -1
    private var yTextureHandle: Int = -1
    private var uTextureHandle: Int = -1
    private var vTextureHandle: Int = -1

    // Quad vertices (normalized device coordinates)
    private val quadVertices = floatArrayOf(
        -1f, -1f, 0f,
         1f, -1f, 0f,
        -1f,  1f, 0f,
         1f,  1f, 0f
    )

    // Texture coordinates (standard YUV mapping)
    private val quadTextures = floatArrayOf(
        0f, 1f,
        1f, 1f,
        0f, 0f,
        1f, 0f
    )

    // Frame data
    private var frameWidth = 0
    private var frameHeight = 0
    private var yPlane = ByteArray(0)
    private var uPlane = ByteArray(0)
    private var vPlane = ByteArray(0)

    // NV21 output buffer
    private var nv21Buffer: ByteArray? = null

    // EGL methods
    private external fun eglGetDisplay(nativeDisplay: Any): EGLDisplay
    private external fun eglInitialize(display: EGLDisplay?, versionMajor: IntArray?, versionMinor: IntArray?): Boolean
    private external fun eglChooseConfig(display: EGLDisplay?, attribList: IntArray?, configs: Array<EGLConfig?>?, configSize: Int, numConfigs: IntArray?): Int
    private external fun eglCreateContext(display: EGLDisplay?, config: EGLConfig?, shareContext: EGLContext?, attribList: IntArray?): EGLContext
    private external fun eglCreatePbufferSurface(display: EGLDisplay?, config: EGLConfig?, attribList: IntArray?): EGLSurface
    private external fun eglMakeCurrent(display: EGLDisplay?, surface: EGLSurface?, read: EGLSurface?, context: EGLContext?): Boolean
    private external fun eglSwapBuffers(display: EGLDisplay?, surface: EGLSurface?): Boolean
    private external fun eglDestroySurface(display: EGLDisplay?, surface: EGLSurface?): Boolean
    private external fun eglDestroyContext(display: EGLDisplay?, context: EGLContext?): Boolean
    private external fun eglTerminate(display: EGLDisplay?): Boolean
    private external fun eglGetError(): Int

    // OpenGL ES 2.0/3.0 methods
    private external fun glCreateShader(type: Int): Int
    private external fun glShaderSource(shader: Int, source: String?)
    private external fun glCompileShader(shader: Int)
    private external fun glCreateProgram(): Int
    private external fun glAttachShader(program: Int, shader: Int)
    private external fun glLinkProgram(program: Int)
    private external fun glUseProgram(program: Int)
    private external fun glGetAttribLocation(program: Int, name: String?): Int
    private external fun glGetUniformLocation(program: Int, name: String?): Int
    private external fun glEnableVertexAttribArray(index: Int)
    private external fun glVertexAttribPointer(
        index: Int, size: Int, type: Int, stride: Int, pointer: Long
    )
    private external fun glDrawArrays(mode: Int, first: Int, count: Int)
    private external fun glGenTextures(n: Int, textures: IntArray?)
    private external fun glDeleteTextures(n: Int, textures: IntArray?)
    private external fun glActiveTexture(texture: Int)
    private external fun glTexImage2D(target: Int, level: Int, internalFormat: Int, width: Int, height: Int, border: Int, format: Int, type: Int, pixels: ByteBuffer?)
    private external fun glTexImage2DWithLong(target: Int, level: Int, internalFormat: Int, width: Int, height: Int, border: Int, format: Int, type: Int, pixels: Long)
    private external fun glTexParameteri(target: Int, pname: Int, param: Int)
    private external fun glReadPixels(x: Int, y: Int, width: Int, height: Int, format: Int, type: Int, pixels: ByteBuffer?)

    init {
        loadNativeLib()
        initializeEgl()
    }

    private fun loadNativeLib() {
        try {
            System.loadLibrary("xCamVirtualCamera")
        } catch (e: UnsatisfiedLinkError) {
            // JNI not available, use reflection-based EGL
            logAction("$TAG: JNI library not found, using reflection")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Initialize EGL context and shaders.
     */
    private fun initializeEgl() {
        try {
            // Create EGL display
            glDisplay = eglGetDisplay(EGL_DEFAULT_DISPLAY)
            if (glDisplay == EGL_NO_DISPLAY) {
                logAction("$TAG: Failed to get EGL display")
                return
            }

            // Initialize EGL
            val versionMajor = IntArray(1)
            val versionMinor = IntArray(1)
            if (!eglInitialize(glDisplay!!, versionMajor, versionMinor)) {
                logAction("$TAG: Failed to initialize EGL")
                return
            }

            // Set EGL attributes for RGB565 surface
            val eglAttribs = intArrayOf(
                EGL_RED_SIZE, 8,
                EGL_GREEN_SIZE, 8,
                EGL_BLUE_SIZE, 8,
                EGL_ALPHA_SIZE, 8,
                EGL_RENDERABLE_TYPE, EGL_OPENGL_ES2_BIT,
                EGL_NONE
            )

            val configs = arrayOfNulls<EGLConfig>(1)
            val numConfigs = IntArray(1)
            eglChooseConfig(glDisplay!!, eglAttribs, configs, 1, numConfigs)

            if (numConfigs[0] == 0 || configs[0] == null) {
                logAction("$TAG: Failed to choose EGL config")
                return
            }

            // Create EGL context (OpenGL ES 2.0/3.0)
            val contextAttribs = intArrayOf(
                EGL_CONTEXT_CLIENT_VERSION, 3,
                EGL_NONE
            )

            glContext = eglCreateContext(
                glDisplay!!, configs[0], EGL_NO_CONTEXT, contextAttribs
            )

            if (glContext == EGL_NO_CONTEXT) {
                logAction("$TAG: Failed to create EGL context")
                return
            }

            // Create EGL surface (pbuffer for offscreen rendering)
            val surfaceAttribs = intArrayOf(
                EGL_WIDTH, 1280,
                EGL_HEIGHT, 720,
                EGL_NONE
            )

            glSurface = eglCreatePbufferSurface(glDisplay!!, configs[0]!!, surfaceAttribs)

            // Make current
            eglMakeCurrent(glDisplay!!, glSurface!!, glSurface!!, glContext!!)

            // Compile and link shaders
            compileAndLinkShaders()

            glInitialized = true
            logAction("$TAG: EGL initialized successfully")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: EGL init failed: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Compile and link the YUV-to-RGB shaders.
     */
    private fun compileAndLinkShaders() {
        try {
            // Compile vertex shader
            val vertexShader = glCreateShader(GL20.GL_VERTEX_SHADER)
            glShaderSource(vertexShader, YUV_VERTEX_SHADER)
            glCompileShader(vertexShader)

            // Compile fragment shader
            val fragmentShader = glCreateShader(GL20.GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER)
            glShaderSource(fragmentShader, YUV_FRAGMENT_SHADER)
            glCompileShader(fragmentShader)

            // Link program
            glProgram = glCreateProgram()
            glAttachShader(glProgram, vertexShader)
            glAttachShader(glProgram, fragmentShader)
            glLinkProgram(glProgram)

            // Get handle locations
            vPositionHandle = glGetAttribLocation(glProgram, "vPosition")
            vTextureCoordHandle = glGetAttribLocation(glProgram, "vTextureCoord")
            yTextureHandle = glGetUniformLocation(glProgram, "yTexture")
            uTextureHandle = glGetUniformLocation(glProgram, "uTexture")
            vTextureHandle = glGetUniformLocation(glProgram, "vTexture")

            logAction("$TAG: Shaders compiled and linked")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Shader compile/link failed: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Render a YUV_420_888 frame to the output surface.
     *
     * @param yPlane Y plane data
     * @param uPlane U plane data
     * @param vPlane V plane data
     * @param width Frame width
     * @param height Frame height
     * @return NV21 byte array ready for output
     */
    fun renderYuvFrame(
        yPlane: ByteArray,
        uPlane: ByteArray,
        vPlane: ByteArray,
        width: Int,
        height: Int
    ): ByteArray? {
        if (!glInitialized || glProgram < 0) {
            // Fallback: direct NV21 copy
            return copyYuvToNv21(yPlane, uPlane, vPlane, width, height)
        }

        this.yPlane = yPlane
        this.uPlane = uPlane
        this.vPlane = vPlane
        this.frameWidth = width
        this.frameHeight = height

        return renderToNv21()
    }

    /**
     * Render an NV21 frame directly to output.
     *
     * @param nv21Data NV21 formatted YUV data
     * @param width Frame width
     * @param height Frame height
     * @return NV21 byte array (same as input if no transformation needed)
     */
    fun renderNv21Frame(nv21Data: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int): ByteArray {
        this.frameWidth = width
        this.frameHeight = height
        val nv21Copy = ByteArray(nv21Data.size)
        System.arraycopy(nv21Data, 0, nv21Copy, 0, nv21Data.size)
        return nv21Copy
    }

    /**
     * Render to NV21 using EGL texture.
     */
    private fun renderToNv21(): ByteArray? {
        try {
            if (!glInitialized || glProgram < 0) return null

            val w = frameWidth
            val h = frameHeight
            val ySize = w * h
            val chromaSize = ySize / 4
            val totalSize = ySize + 2 * chromaSize

            // Allocate output buffer
            val outputBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(totalSize).apply {
                order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder())
            }

            // Set up YUV textures
            val textures = IntArray(3)
            glGenTextures(3, textures)

            // Y plane (luminance)
            val yTexture = textures[0]
            glActiveTexture(0x0DE0) // GL_TEXTURE0
            glBindTexture(0x8063) // GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES
            glTexParameteri(0x8063, 0x2801, 0x2000) // GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR
            glTexParameteri(0x8063, 0x2800, 0x2001) // GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR
            glTexParameteri(0x8063, 0x2100, 0x2000) // GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE
            glTexParameteri(0x8063, 0x2101, 0x2000) // GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE

            // Upload Y plane to texture
            glTexImage2D(
                0x8063, // GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES
                0, // level
                0x1900, // GL_LUMINANCE
                w, h, 0,
                0x1900, // GL_LUMINANCE
                0x1401, // GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE
                yPlane.asByteBuffer()
            )

            // U plane (chrominance)
            val uTexture = textures[1]
            glActiveTexture(0x0DE1) // GL_TEXTURE1
            glTexImage2D(
                0x8063, 0, 0x1900, w / 2, h / 2, 0,
                0x1900, 0x1401, uPlane.asByteBuffer()
            )

            // V plane (chrominance)
            val vTexture = textures[2]
            glActiveTexture(0x0DE2) // GL_TEXTURE2
            glTexImage2D(
                0x8063, 0, 0x1900, w / 2, h / 2, 0,
                0x1900, 0x1401, vPlane.asByteBuffer()
            )

            // Set up vertex data
            val vertexBuffer = java.nio.FloatBuffer.allocate(12).apply {
                put(quadVertices)
                position(0)
            }

            val texCoordBuffer = java.nio.FloatBuffer.allocate(8).apply {
                put(quadTextures)
                position(0)
            }

            // Use shader program
            glUseProgram(glProgram)

            // Set vertex attributes
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(vPositionHandle)
            glVertexAttribPointer(vPositionHandle, 3, 0x1406, 0, 12, vertexBuffer)

            glEnableVertexAttribArray(vTextureCoordHandle)
            glVertexAttribPointer(vTextureCoordHandle, 2, 0x1406, 0, 8, texCoordBuffer)

            // Set texture uniforms
            glUseProgram(glProgram)
            glActiveTexture(0x0DE0)
            glUniform1i(yTextureHandle, 0)
            glBindTexture(0x8063, yTexture)

            glActiveTexture(0x0DE1)
            glUniform1i(uTextureHandle, 1)
            glBindTexture(0x8063, uTexture)

            glActiveTexture(0x0DE2)
            glUniform1i(vTextureHandle, 2)
            glBindTexture(0x8063, vTexture)

            // Draw the quad
            glDrawArrays(0x0003, 0, 4) // GL_TRIANGLE_STRIP

            // Read pixels
            glReadPixels(0, 0, w, h, 0x1907, 0x1401, outputBuffer)

            // Clean up textures
            glDeleteTextures(3, textures)

            val result = ByteArray(outputBuffer.remaining())
            outputBuffer.get(result)
            return result

        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Render to NV21 failed: ${e.message}")
            return null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Convert YUV planes to NV21 format (direct copy, no shader).
     */
    private fun copyYuvToNv21(yPlane: ByteArray, uPlane: ByteArray, vPlane: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int): ByteArray {
        val ySize = width * height
        val chromaSize = ySize / 4
        val totalSize = ySize + 2 * chromaSize
        val nv21 = ByteArray(totalSize)

        // Copy Y plane
        System.arraycopy(yPlane, 0, nv21, 0, minOf(yPlane.size, ySize))

        // Swap U and V planes for NV21 (NV21 = Y + VU interleaved)
        val nv21Offset = ySize
        val minUSize = minOf(uPlane.size, chromaSize)
        val minVSize = minOf(vPlane.size, chromaSize)
        for (i in 0 until minUSize) {
            nv21[nv21Offset + i * 2 + 1] = uPlane[i]
            nv21[nv21Offset + i * 2] = vPlane[i]
        }

        return nv21
    }

    /**
     * Convert YUV planes to JPEG bytes.
     */
    fun toJpeg(yPlane: ByteArray, uPlane: ByteArray, vPlane: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int, quality: Int = 95): ByteArray? {
        val nv21 = copyYuvToNv21(yPlane, uPlane, vPlane, width, height)
        return nv21ToJpeg(nv21, width, height, quality)
    }

    /**
     * Convert NV21 to JPEG bytes.
     */
    private fun nv21ToJpeg(nv21: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int, quality: Int): ByteArray? {
        try {
            val yuvImage = YuvImage(nv21, ImageFormat.NV21, width, height, null)
            val out = java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()
            return if (yuvImage.compressToJpeg(Rect(0, 0, width, height), quality, out)) {
                out.toByteArray()
            } else {
                null
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: NV21 to JPEG failed: ${e.message}")
            return null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get NV21 size for given dimensions.
     */
    fun getNv21Size(width: Int, height: Int): Int {
        val ySize = width * height
        val chromaSize = ySize / 4
        return ySize + 2 * chromaSize
    }

    /**
     * Check if EGL is initialized.
     */
    fun isInitialized(): Boolean = glInitialized

    /**
     * Release EGL resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        try {
            if (glInitialized) {
                eglMakeCurrent(
                    glDisplay, EGL_NO_SURFACE, EGL_NO_SURFACE,
                    EGL_NO_CONTEXT
                )
                glSurface?.let { eglDestroySurface(glDisplay!!, it) }
                glContext?.let { eglDestroyContext(glDisplay!!, it) }
                eglTerminate(glDisplay)
            }
            glInitialized = false
            logAction("$TAG: EGL released")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: EGL release failed: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    // Extension: convert ByteArray to ByteBuffer for glTexImage2D
    private fun ByteArray.asByteBuffer(): ByteBuffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(this).apply {
        position(0)
        limit(size)
    }

    private fun minOf(a: Int, b: Int): Int = if (a < b) a else b
}
```

```kt
/**
 * Configuration constants for the Camera2 HAL3 Virtual Camera.
 * Implements the Android Virtual Camera stack (AOSP hardware/interfaces/camera/).
 */
object VirtualCameraConstants {

    /**
     * Camera facing direction for the virtual camera.
     * EXTERNAL (2) = external/USB/virtual camera per CameraCharacteristics
     */
    const val VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING = android.hardware.Camera2.CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_EXTERNAL

    /**
     * Camera ID used to register with the CameraServer.
     * Typically camera ID "2" or "3" (after physical cameras 0 and 1).
     */
    const val VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID = "2"

    /**
     * Hardware level reported by the virtual camera.
     * FULL = supports all Camera2 API features (most important for KYC bypass).
     * EXTERNAL = USB/virtual camera level (limited 3A, no flash).
     */
    const val VIRTUAL_HARDWARE_LEVEL = android.hardware.Camera2.CameraCharacteristics.HARDWARE_LEVEL_FULL

    /**
     * Sensor info - this is a virtual/external sensor
     */
    const val VIRTUAL_SENSOR_ACTIVE_AE_AVAILABLE = true
    const val VIRTUAL_SENSOR_ACTIVE_AF_AVAILABLE = true
    const val VIRTUAL_SENSOR_ACTIVE_AWB_AVAILABLE = true

    const val VIRTUAL_MAX_STREAMS = 4

    const val VIRTUAL_DEFAULT_WIDTH = 1280
```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/core/virtualcamera/VirtualCameraDevice.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.core.virtualcamera

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCaptureSession
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraDevice
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest
import android.hardware.camera2.TotalCaptureResult
import android.media.Image
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.util.Size
import android.util.SparseIntArray
import android.view.Surface
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.capture.CaptureManager
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.capture.MediaCodecYuvDecoder
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.engine.MediaEngine
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.engine.XCamEngine
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.settings.SettingsManager
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.surface.SurfaceManager
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants
import com.hazbu.xcam.utils.Logger
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/**
 * Virtual Camera HAL3 Device.
 *
 * Represents a single virtual camera device from the CameraServer's perspective.
 * Handles opening the device, creating capture sessions, and routing capture
 * requests to the appropriate output surfaces.
 *
 * This is called by the CameraService when an app calls:
 *   CameraManager.openCamera("2", callback, handler)
 *
 * The returned object implements:
 *   - CameraDevice (for device state management)
 *   - createCaptureSession() → VirtualCameraSession
 *   - createCaptureRequest() → CaptureRequest.Builder
 *   - capture() / setRepeatingRequest() / captureBurst()
 */
@SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
class VirtualCameraDevice(
    private val deviceId: String,
    private val deviceFacing: Int,
    private val streamConfigs: android.hardware.camera2.params.StreamConfigurationMap,
    private val eglRenderer: EglFrameRenderer,
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit,
    private val handler: Handler
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xVirtCamDev"

        // Default preview orientation
        private val ORIENTATION_MAP = SparseIntArray().apply {
            put(0, 90)
            put(90, 0)
            put(180, 270)
            put(270, 180)
        }
    }

    // State
    private var isInitialized = false
    private var isDestroyed = false
    private val activeSessions = CopyOnWriteArrayList<VirtualCameraSession>()

    // Camera state callback (set by caller)
    private var stateCallback: CameraDevice.StateCallback? = null

    // Settings
    private var isMirrored = false
    private var rotationAngle = 0
    private var mediaPath: String? = null

    // Video decoder for virtual camera feed
    private var yuvDecoder: MediaCodecYuvDecoder? = null
    private var mediaEngine: MediaEngine? = null
    private var captureManager: CaptureManager? = null

    // Surface manager for tracking input/output surfaces
    private val surfaceManager = SurfaceManager { msg -> logAction("$TAG: $msg") }

    // Capture state
    private var isCapturing = false
    private var currentCaptureWidth = 1280
    private var currentCaptureHeight = 720

    // Handler for camera operations
    private val cameraHandler = handler

    /**
     * Initialize the virtual camera device.
     */
    fun initialize() {
        isInitialized = true
        logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera device initialized (ID: $deviceId, Facing: $deviceFacing)")
    }

    /**
     * Set the state callback.
     */
    fun setStateCallback(callback: CameraDevice.StateCallback) {
        stateCallback = callback
        // Report success immediately (virtual camera is always "ready")
        cameraHandler.post {
            callback.onOpened(this)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Create a capture session with the given output surfaces.
     * This is called by the Camera2 framework when an app creates a capture session.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun createCaptureSession(
        outputSurfaces: List<Surface>,
        sessionCallback: CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback,
        handler: Handler?
    ): VirtualCameraSession {
        val session = VirtualCameraSession(
            deviceId,
            outputSurfaces,
            streamConfigs,
            eglRenderer,
            { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Session: $msg") },
            cameraHandler,
            sessionCallback,
            handler
        )

        activeSessions.add(session)
        logAction("$TAG: Created capture session with ${outputSurfaces.size} output surfaces")
        return session
    }

    /**
     * Create a capture request for the given template.
     */
    fun createCaptureRequest(template: Int): CaptureRequest.Builder {
        return CaptureRequest.Builder(
            android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE,
            template
        ).apply {
            // Set default controls
            set(android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_MODE, 1) // ENABLED
            set(android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE,
                android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE)
            set(android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE,
                android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE_ON)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Capture a single request.
     */
    fun capture(requests: Array<out CaptureRequest>, callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?,
                handler: Handler?) {
        // Route to active sessions
        for (session in activeSessions) {
            session.capture(requests, callback, handler)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Set up repeating requests.
     */
    fun setRepeatingRequest(request: CaptureRequest, callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?,
                            handler: Handler?) {
        for (session in activeSessions) {
            session.setRepeatingRequest(request, callback, handler)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Set up repeating requests for a burst.
     */
    fun captureBurst(requests: List<out CaptureRequest>, callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?,
                     handler: Handler?) {
        for (session in activeSessions) {
            session.captureBurst(requests, callback, handler)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the camera ID.
     */
    fun getId(): String = deviceId

    /**
     * Get the camera facing direction.
     */
    fun getFacing(): Int = deviceFacing

    /**
     * Check if the device is initialized.
     */
    fun isInitialized(): Boolean = isInitialized

    /**
     * Check if the device is destroyed.
     */
    fun isDestroyed(): Boolean = isDestroyed

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun release() {
        isDestroyed = true
        for (session in activeSessions.toList()) {
            session.release()
        }
        activeSessions.clear()
        yuvDecoder?.stop()
        mediaEngine?.stop()
        logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera device released")
    }

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera media path.
     */
    fun setMediaPath(path: String?) {
        this.mediaPath = path
        if (!path.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            // Start video decoder for the virtual camera
            startVideoDecoder(path)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera mirror state.
     */
    fun setMirrored(mirrored: Boolean) {
        this.isMirrored = mirrored
    }

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera rotation angle.
     */
    fun setRotationAngle(angle: Int) {
        this.rotationAngle = angle
    }

    /**
     * Start the video decoder for the virtual camera feed.
     */
    private fun startVideoDecoder(path: String) {
        yuvDecoder?.stop()
        mediaEngine?.stop()

        // Start the video engine
        mediaEngine = MediaEngine { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Engine: $msg") }

        // Start the video decoder
        yuvDecoder = MediaCodecYuvDecoder(
            android.content.Context::class.java.classLoader
                ?.loadClass("android.content.Context")?.getDeclaredField("CONTEXT_SERVICE")
                ?.get(null) as? android.content.Context
                ?: android.content.Context.createApplicationContext(),
            path,
            { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Decoder: $msg") }
        )

        // Get the context from the handler
        val context = try {
            val activityThread = Class.forName("android.app.ActivityThread")
            val currentApplication = activityThread.getMethod("currentApplication")
                .invoke(null) as? android.app.Application
            currentApplication ?: android.content.Context.createApplicationContext()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            android.content.Context.createApplicationContext()
        }

        yuvDecoder = MediaCodecYuvDecoder(
            context,
            path,
            { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Decoder: $msg") }
        )
        yuvDecoder?.start()
        logAction("$TAG: Video decoder started for path: $path")
    }

    /**
     * Get the latest YUV frame from the video decoder.
     */
    fun getLatestYuvFrame(): MediaCodecYuvDecoder.YuvFrame? {
        return yuvDecoder?.latestFrame
    }

    /**
     * Check if the virtual camera is active (has a valid video feed).
     */
    fun isActive(): Boolean = isInitialized && mediaPath != null

    /**
     * Get the current capture dimensions.
     */
    fun getCaptureDimensions(): Pair<Int, Int> {
        return Pair(currentCaptureWidth, currentCaptureHeight)
    }

    /**
     * Set the current capture dimensions.
     */
    fun setCaptureDimensions(width: Int, height: Int) {
        this.currentCaptureWidth = width
        this.currentCaptureHeight = height
    }

    /**
     * Get the current capture size.
     */
    fun getCaptureSize(): Int {
        return currentCaptureWidth * currentCaptureHeight
    }

    /**
     * Get the stream configuration map.
     */
    fun getStreamConfigs(): android.hardware.camera2.params.StreamConfigurationMap {
        return streamConfigs
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device is valid (initialized and not destroyed).
     */
    fun isValid(): Boolean = isInitialized && !isDestroyed

    /**
     * Get the number of active sessions.
     */
    fun getActiveSessionCount(): Int = activeSessions.size
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/core/virtualcamera/VirtualCameraManager.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.core.virtualcamera

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import android.util.Size
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants
import com.hazbu.xcam.utils.Logger
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/**
 * Virtual Camera Manager - High-level coordinator for the Camera2 HAL3 virtual camera.
 *
 * This class:
 * 1. Registers the virtual camera with the Android CameraServer
 * 2. Manages the virtual camera lifecycle (start/stop)
 * 3. Provides the virtual camera status to other xCam components
 * 4. Handles frame rendering for the virtual camera feed
 *
 * Usage from XCamModule:
 *   val virtCam = VirtualCameraManager(context)
 *   virtCam.start()
 *   // ... app opens camera, receives virtual feed
 *   virtCam.stop()
 *
 * Architecture:
 * ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  VirtualCameraManager (this class)           │
 * │  • Registers/Unregisters with CameraServer    │
 * │  • Manages VirtualCameraProvider             │
 * │  • Manages EglFrameRenderer                  │
 * │  • Provides status to xCamModule             │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 *           │
 * ┌─────────▼───────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  VirtualCameraProvider (HAL3 entry point)    │
 * │  • Registers with CameraManager              │
 * │  • Reports EXTERNAL hardware level           │
 * │  • Reports all supported stream configs      │
 * │  • Creates VirtualCameraDevice on open       │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 *           │
 * ┌─────────▼───────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  VirtualCameraDevice (per-camera instance)   │
 * │  • Creates capture sessions                  │
 * │  • Manages capture requests                  │
 * │  • Routes output to VirtualCameraSession     │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 *           │
 * ┌─────────▼───────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  VirtualCameraSession (per-capture)          │
 * │  • Handles capture callbacks                 │
 * │  • Renders frames via EglFrameRenderer       │
 * │  • Sends capture results to client           │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 *           │
 * ┌─────────▼───────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  EglFrameRenderer (frame rendering)          │
 * │  • EGL context/shaders for YUV→RGB           │
 * │  • Renders YUV_420_888 frames to NV21/JPEG   │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 */
@SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
class VirtualCameraManager(
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit
) {
    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xVirtCamMgr"
        private const val VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID = "2"
        private const val VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING = CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_EXTERNAL

        // Virtual camera capabilities
        private val SUPPORTED_STREAM_TYPES = intArrayOf(
            android.graphics.ImageFormat.YUV_420_888,
            android.graphics.ImageFormat.NV21,
            android.graphics.ImageFormat.JPEG
        )
    }

    // State
    private var isStarted = false
    private var isPaused = false

    // Components
    private val provider = VirtualCameraProvider { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Provider: $msg") }
    private val eglRenderer = EglFrameRenderer { msg -> logAction("$TAG: EGL: $msg") }

    // Settings
    private var mediaPath: String? = null
    private var isMirrored = false
    private var rotationAngle = 0

    // Listener
    private var statusListener: StatusListener? = null

    /**
     * Start the virtual camera.
     * This registers the virtual camera with the Android CameraServer.
     */
    fun start() {
        if (isStarted) {
            logAction("$TAG: Already started")
            return
        }

        try {
            // Register with CameraServer
            provider.register()
            isStarted = true

            logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera started successfully")
            logAction("$TAG: Status: ${provider.getStatus()}")

            // Notify listener
            statusListener?.onStatusChanged(true)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to start virtual camera: ${e.message}")
            isStarted = false
            statusListener?.onStatusChanged(false)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Stop the virtual camera.
     * This unregisters the virtual camera from the Android CameraServer.
     */
    fun stop() {
        if (!isStarted) {
            logAction("$TAG: Already stopped")
            return
        }

        try {
            provider.unregister()
            isStarted = false
            logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera stopped")

            // Notify listener
            statusListener?.onStatusChanged(false)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to stop virtual camera: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the virtual camera is started.
     */
    fun isStarted(): Boolean = isStarted

    /**
     * Check if the virtual camera is active (started and not paused).
     */
    fun isActive(): Boolean = isStarted && !isPaused

    /**
     * Pause the virtual camera (keep registered, but stop rendering).
     */
    fun pause() {
        isPaused = true
        logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera paused")
    }

    /**
     * Resume the virtual camera (start rendering again).
     */
    fun resume() {
        isPaused = false
        logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera resumed")
    }

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera's current status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String = provider.getStatus()

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera's enabled state.
     */
    fun isEnabled(): Boolean = isStarted && !isPaused

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera media path.
     */
    fun setMediaPath(path: String?) {
        this.mediaPath = path
        logAction("$TAG: Media path set to: $path")
    }

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera media path.
     */
    fun getMediaPath(): String? = mediaPath

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera mirror state.
     */
    fun setMirrored(mirrored: Boolean) {
        this.isMirrored = mirrored
        logAction("$TAG: Mirror state set to: $mirrored")
    }

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera mirror state.
     */
    fun isMirrored(): Boolean = isMirrored

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera rotation angle.
     */
    fun setRotationAngle(angle: Int) {
        this.rotationAngle = angle
        logAction("$TAG: Rotation angle set to: $angle")
    }

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera rotation angle.
     */
    fun getRotationAngle(): Int = rotationAngle

    /**
     * Check if the virtual camera is ready to render frames.
     */
    fun isReadyForRendering(): Boolean = isStarted && !isPaused && mediaPath != null

    /**
     * Get the EGL renderer.
     */
    fun getEglRenderer(): EglFrameRenderer = eglRenderer

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera provider.
     */
    fun getProvider(): VirtualCameraProvider = provider

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        stop()
        eglRenderer.release()
        logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera manager released")
    }

    /**
     * Set the status listener.
     */
    fun setStatusListener(listener: StatusListener?) {
        this.statusListener = listener
    }

    /**
     * Interface for virtual camera status changes.
     */
    interface StatusListener {
        fun onStatusChanged(isActive: Boolean)
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/core/virtualcamera/VirtualCameraProvider.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.core.virtualcamera

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraDevice
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.TotalCaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.params.StreamConfigurationMap
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.util.Log
import android.util.Size
import android.view.Surface
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants
import com.hazbu.xcam.utils.Logger
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/**
 * Virtual Camera HAL3 Provider.
 *
 * This is the entry point for the Camera2 HAL. It registers with the Android
 * CameraServer (via CameraManager) as an external/USB camera device that
 * can serve all the same capabilities as a physical camera.
 *
 * Architecture:
 * ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  CameraService (system)                      │
 * │  openCamera("2") → returns VirtualCameraDevice │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 *           │
 * ┌─────────▼───────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  VirtualCameraProvider (this class)          │
 * │  • Registers with CameraManager              │
 * │  • Reports EXTERNAL hardware level           │
 * │  • Reports all supported stream configs      │
 * │  • Creates VirtualCameraDevice on open       │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 *           │
 * ┌─────────▼───────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  VirtualCameraDevice (per-camera instance)   │
 * │  • Creates capture sessions                  │
 * │  • Manages capture requests                  │
 * │  • Routes output to VirtualCameraSession     │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 *           │
 * ┌─────────▼───────────────────────────────────┐
 * │  VirtualCameraSession (per-session)          │
 * │  • Handles capture callbacks                 │
 * │  • Renders frames via EglFrameRenderer       │
 * │  • Sends capture results to client           │
 * └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘
 */
@SuppressLint("MissingPermission", "UnsafeExperimentalAccessibilityApiUsage")
class VirtualCameraProvider(
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit
) {
    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xVirtCamProv"
        private const val VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID = "2"
        private const val VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING = CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_EXTERNAL
    }

    private val cameraManager: CameraManager? = try {
        android.content.Context.CAMERA_SERVICE?.let {
            it.getSystemService(CameraManager::class.java)
        }
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        null
    }

    private var isRegistered = false
    private var isExternalCameraRegistered = false
    private val registeredDevices = mutableListOf<String>()
    private val deviceListeners = CopyOnWriteArrayList<DeviceListener>()

    // Handler thread for camera operations
    private val cameraThread = HandlerThread("xCam-VirtCam", android.os.Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_DISPLAY).apply {
        start()
    }
    private val cameraHandler = Handler(cameraThread.looper)

    // Virtual camera device
    private var virtualDevice: VirtualCameraDevice? = null

    // EGL renderer for frame rendering
    private val eglRenderer = EglFrameRenderer { msg -> logAction("$TAG: $msg") }

    /**
     * Register the virtual camera with the CameraManager.
     * This makes it appear as an external/USB camera device.
     */
    fun register() {
        if (isRegistered) {
            logAction("$TAG: Already registered")
            return
        }

        try {
            cameraManager?.let { cm ->
                // Register as an external/USB camera
                cm.registerVirtualCamera(VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING, object :
                    CameraManager.VirtualCameraDevice {

                    override fun getCharacteristics(): CameraCharacteristics {
                        return buildCameraCharacteristics()
                    }

                    override fun createDevice(): CameraDevice {
                        return VirtualCameraDevice(
                            VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID,
                            VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING,
                            buildStreamConfigs(),
                            eglRenderer,
                            { msg -> logAction("$TAG: $msg") },
                            cameraHandler
                        ).apply {
                            virtualDevice = this
                        }
                    }

                    override fun getPhysicalCameraDeviceIds(): Array<String> {
                        return emptyArray()
                    }
                })

                isRegistered = true
                isExternalCameraRegistered = true
                logAction("$TAG: Registered virtual camera ID=$VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID facing=EXTERNAL")
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to register virtual camera: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Unregister the virtual camera from the CameraManager.
     */
    fun unregister() {
        try {
            cameraManager?.unregisterVirtualCamera(VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID)
            isRegistered = false
            isExternalCameraRegistered = false
            logAction("$TAG: Unregistered virtual camera ID=$VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to unregister virtual camera: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the virtual camera is registered.
     */
    fun isRegistered(): Boolean = isRegistered

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera's current status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String = when {
        isRegistered -> "REGISTERED (ID: $VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID, FACING: EXTERNAL)"
        isExternalCameraRegistered -> "REGISTERED AS EXTERNAL"
        else -> "NOT REGISTERED"
    }

    /**
     * Build CameraCharacteristics for the virtual camera.
     * This tells the Camera2 framework what capabilities the virtual camera has.
     */
    private fun buildCameraCharacteristics(): CameraCharacteristics {
        return CameraCharacteristics(VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID).apply {
            // Camera facing: EXTERNAL (2) = external/USB/virtual
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING)

            // Hardware level: FULL (supports all Camera2 features)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.HARDWARE_LEVEL, CameraMetadata.HARDWARE_LEVEL_FULL)

            // Info: this is a virtual/external camera
            set(CameraCharacteristics.INFO_SUPPORTED_HARDWARE_LEVEL, CameraMetadata.HARDWARE_LEVEL_FULL)

            // Sensor: external
            set(CameraCharacteristicsSENSOR_PHYSICAL_SIZE, 1.0f) // 1 inch (arbitrary for virtual)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.NOISE_PROFILE, floatArrayOf(0f, 0f))

            // Optical: external
            set(CameraCharacteristics.opticalLENS_FACING, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING)

            // Info: external camera
            set(CameraCharacteristics.INFO_USING_DYNAMIC_VIDEO_STABILIZATION_MODE, 1)

            // Request: supported
            set(CameraCharacteristics.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES, arrayOf(
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_BUNDLE,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_BurstCapture,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_CALIBRated_LENS,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_CONSTANT_VIDEO_STABILIZATION_MODE,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_DYNAMIC_RANGE_10_BIT,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_DYNAMIC_RANGE_HIGH,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_DYNAMIC_RANGE_WIDE,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_FIXED_DEVICE_Focus,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_FLASH,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_Focus_Area,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_LOGICAL_MULTI_CAMERA,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIESManual_LeNS_MANAGEMENT,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_NATIVE,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_PARALLEL_OUTPUT,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_PER_FRAME_DATA_PIPELINE,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_POST_PROCESSING_RATE,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_RAW,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_RAW_DEPTH,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_SEmi_RAw,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_SHarpness,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_Slow_MOTION,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_SNAPSHOT_HDR,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_STEREO_CAMERA,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_STEREO_VIDEO,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_SYNC,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_TURBO_3A,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_UPSTREAM_PROCESSED,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_VENDOR,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_YCRCB_420,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_YUV_restRictED,
                CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_ZSL,
            ))

            // Stream configuration map
            val configs = buildStreamConfigs()
            set(
                CameraCharacteristics.SCALER_STREAM_CONFIGURATION_MAP,
                configs
            )

            // Hot standby: not supported
            set(CameraCharacteristics.INFO_AVAILABLE_HOT_STANDBY, 0)

            // Max zoom: 16x
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING)

            // Min focal length: 1.0
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING)

            // Max focal length: 5.0
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, VIRTUAL_CAMERA_FACING)

            // Distortion model: none (virtual camera has no optical distortion)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.DISTORTION_MODEL, 1.0f)

            // Distortion transform: identity matrix
            set(CameraCharacteristics.DISTORTION_TRANSFORM, floatArrayOf(
                1.0f, 0f, 0f, 0f,
                0f, 1.0f, 0f, 0f,
                0f, 0f, 1.0f, 0f,
                0f, 0f, 0f, 1.0f
            ))

            // Sensor timing: continuous (virtual camera can deliver frames instantly)
            set(CameraCharacteristicsSENSOR_STATIC_SENSOR_TIMING, 0L)

            // Hot standby: not supported
            set(CameraCharacteristics.INFO_AVAILABLE_HOT_STANDBY, 0)

            // Logical multi-camera: not supported (single virtual camera)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LOGICAL_MULTI_CAMERA_ACTIVEPHYSICAL_CAMES, emptyArray())

            // Max num of active logical camera devices
            set(CameraCharacteristics.INFO_SUPPORTED_NAMED_IMAGES, emptyArray())

            // Max num of active logical camera devices
            set(CameraCharacteristics.INFO_SUPPORTED_NAMED_IMAGES, emptyArray())
        }
    }

    /**
     * Build StreamConfigurationMap with supported output sizes.
     * This tells the Camera2 framework what stream sizes this virtual camera supports.
     */
    private fun buildStreamConfigs(): StreamConfigurationMap {
        val outputSizes = arrayOf(
            Size(640, 480),
            Size(1280, 720),
            Size(1920, 1080),
            Size(2560, 1440),
            Size(3840, 2160),
            Size(4096, 2160),
            Size(4096, 2160)
        )

        // Input sizes (same as output for virtual camera)
        val inputSizes = arrayOf(
            Size(1920, 1080),
            Size(1280, 720),
            Size(640, 480)
        )

        return StreamConfigurationMap(
            outputSizes,
            inputSizes,
            arrayOf(
                android.graphics.ImageFormat.YUV_420_888,
                android.graphics.ImageFormat.NV21,
                android.graphics.ImageFormat.JPEG
            ),
            emptyArray() // No internal formats
        )
    }

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera's EGL renderer.
     */
    fun getEglRenderer(): EglFrameRenderer = eglRenderer

    /**
     * Get the virtual camera's status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String = when {
        isRegistered -> "REGISTERED (ID: $VIRTUAL_CAMERA_ID, FACING: EXTERNAL)"
        isExternalCameraRegistered -> "REGISTERED AS EXTERNAL"
        else -> "NOT REGISTERED"
    }

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        unregister()
        eglRenderer.release()
        cameraThread.quitSafely()
        virtualDevice?.release()
        virtualDevice = null
        logAction("$TAG: Virtual camera provider released")
    }

    /**
     * Interface for virtual camera device lifecycle events.
     */
    interface DeviceListener {
        fun onCameraOpened(deviceId: String)
        fun onCameraClosed(deviceId: String)
        fun onCameraError(deviceId: String, error: Int)
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/core/virtualcamera/VirtualCameraSession.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.core.virtualcamera

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCaptureSession
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraDevice
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.TotalCaptureResult
import android.media.Image
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.util.Log
import android.util.Size
import android.view.Surface
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.capture.CaptureManager
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.capture.MediaCodecYuvDecoder
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.capture.YuvFrameProcessor
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.engine.MediaEngine
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.engine.XCamEngine
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.settings.SettingsManager
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.surface.SurfaceManager
import com.hazbu.xcam.core.surface.SurfaceProvider
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants
import com.hazbu.xcam.utils.Logger
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList

/**
 * Virtual Camera HAL3 Capture Session.
 *
 * Handles a single capture session for the virtual camera. Manages:
 * - Capture requests (single, repeating, burst)
 * - Frame rendering (YUV→JPEG for stills, YUV for preview)
 * - Capture results (sent back to the client app via callback)
 *
 * This is called when an app creates a capture session:
 *   CameraDevice.createCaptureSession(surfaces, callback, handler)
 */
@SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
class VirtualCameraSession(
    private val deviceId: String,
    private val outputSurfaces: List<Surface>,
    private val streamConfigs: android.hardware.camera2.params.StreamConfigurationMap,
    private val eglRenderer: EglFrameRenderer,
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit,
    private val handler: Handler,
    private val stateCallback: CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback,
    private val sessionHandler: Handler?
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xVirtCamSess"
    }

    // State
    private var isActive = false
    private var isRepeating = false
    private var isPaused = false

    // Capture state
    private var currentCaptureWidth = 1280
    private var currentCaptureHeight = 720

    // Frame rendering state
    private var isCapturing = false
    private var captureCount = 0
    private var lastCaptureTime = 0L

    // Settings
    private var isMirrored = false
    private var rotationAngle = 0

    // Video decoder for virtual camera feed
    private var yuvDecoder: MediaCodecYuvDecoder? = null

    // Capture manager for still captures
    private val captureManager = CaptureManager(
        { context -> context },
        { ctx -> },
        { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Capture: $msg") }
    )

    // EGL renderer for frame rendering
    private val yuvProcessor = YuvFrameProcessor()

    // Surface manager for tracking input/output surfaces
    private val surfaceManager = SurfaceManager { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Surface: $msg") }

    // Handler thread for capture operations
    private val captureThread = HandlerThread("xCam-Capture", android.os.Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_DISPLAY).apply {
        start()
    }
    private val captureHandler = Handler(captureThread.looper)

    // State callback
    private var captureCallback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback? = null
    private var repeatRequestCallback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback? = null

    // Current capture state
    private var currentCaptureRequest: android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest? = null

    // Surface tracking
    private val previewSurfaces = mutableSetOf<Surface>()
    private val imageReaderSurfaces = mutableSetOf<Surface>()
    private val videoSurfaces = mutableSetOf<Surface>()

    init {
        // Initialize surfaces
        for (surface in outputSurfaces) {
            if (surface.isValid) {
                surfaceManager.registerPreviewSurface(surface)
                previewSurfaces.add(surface)
            }
        }

        isActive = true
        stateCallback.onConfigured(this)
        logAction("$TAG: Session created with ${outputSurfaces.size} surfaces")
    }

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera media path.
     */
    fun setMediaPath(path: String?) {
        if (!path.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            // Start video decoder
            yuvDecoder = MediaCodecYuvDecoder(
                android.content.Context.createApplicationContext(),
                path,
                { msg -> logAction("$TAG: Decoder: $msg") }
            )
            yuvDecoder?.start()
            logAction("$TAG: Video decoder started for path: $path")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera mirror state.
     */
    fun setMirrored(mirrored: Boolean) {
        this.isMirrored = mirrored
    }

    /**
     * Set the virtual camera rotation angle.
     */
    fun setRotationAngle(angle: Int) {
        this.rotationAngle = angle
    }

    /**
     * Capture a single request.
     */
    fun capture(requests: Array<out android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest>,
                callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?, handler: Handler?) {
        for (request in requests) {
            captureSingle(request, callback, handler)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Set up repeating requests (for preview).
     */
    fun setRepeatingRequest(request: android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest,
                           callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?,
                           handler: Handler?) {
        this.captureCallback = callback
        this.isRepeating = true
        logAction("$TAG: Repeating request enabled")
    }

    /**
     * Capture a burst of requests.
     */
    fun captureBurst(requests: List<out android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest>,
                     callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?, handler: Handler?) {
        for (request in requests) {
            captureSingle(request, callback, handler)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Capture a single request and render the virtual camera frame.
     */
    private fun captureSingle(request: android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest,
                              callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?,
                              handler: Handler?) {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        if (now - lastCaptureTime < 33) return // ~30fps max

        lastCaptureTime = now
        captureCount++

        // Get the latest YUV frame from the video decoder
        val yuvFrame = yuvDecoder?.latestFrame ?: return

        // Render the frame using EGL
        val nv21 = eglRenderer.renderYuvFrame(
            yuvFrame.yPlane,
            yuvFrame.uPlane,
            yuvFrame.vPlane,
            yuvFrame.width,
            yuvFrame.height
        ) ?: return

        // Apply rotation and mirror
        val finalNv21 = if (rotationAngle != 0 || isMirrored) {
            applyTransformation(nv21, yuvFrame.width, yuvFrame.height, rotationAngle, isMirrored)
        } else {
            nv21
        }

        // Send to output surfaces
        for (surface in outputSurfaces) {
            if (surface.isValid && surface.isCapturing) {
                // Write NV21 frame to the surface
                writeFrameToSurface(surface, finalNv21, yuvFrame.width, yuvFrame.height)
            }
        }

        // Send capture result
        sendCaptureResult(request, callback, handler, finalNv21.size)
    }

    /**
     * Apply rotation and mirror transformation.
     */
    private fun applyTransformation(
        nv21: ByteArray,
        width: Int,
        height: Int,
        rotation: Int,
        mirrored: Boolean
    ): ByteArray {
        var result = nv21

        if (rotation != 0) {
            result = rotateNv21(result, width, height, rotation)
        }

        if (mirrored) {
            result = mirrorNv21(result, width, height)
        }

        return result
    }

    /**
     * Rotate NV21 data.
     */
    private fun rotateNv21(nv21: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int, degrees: Int): ByteArray {
        // Simple 90-degree rotation
        val rotatedWidth = if (degrees == 90 || degrees == 270) height else width
        val rotatedHeight = if (degrees == 90 || degrees == 270) width else height
        val rotatedSize = rotatedWidth * rotatedHeight + 2 * (rotatedWidth * rotatedHeight / 4)
        val rotated = ByteArray(rotatedSize)

        val ySize = width * height
        val chromaSize = ySize / 4

        // Rotate Y plane
        for (y in 0 until rotatedHeight) {
            for (x in 0 until rotatedWidth) {
                val srcX = if (degrees == 90) y else (rotatedWidth - 1 - x)
                val srcY = if (degrees == 90) rotatedWidth - 1 - x else y
                if (srcX < width && srcY < height) {
                    rotated[y * rotatedWidth + x] = nv21[srcY * width + srcX]
                }
            }
        }

        // Rotate VU plane (NV21 stores VU interleaved)
        val dstVOffset = rotatedWidth * rotatedHeight
        val srcVOffset = ySize
        val srcChromaSize = chromaSize
        for (i in 0 until srcChromaSize) {
            if (dstVOffset + i * 2 + 1 < rotatedSize) {
                rotated[dstVOffset + i * 2] = nv21[srcVOffset + i * 2]
                rotated[dstVOffset + i * 2 + 1] = nv21[srcVOffset + i * 2 + 1]
            }
        }

        return rotated
    }

    /**
     * Mirror NV21 data.
     */
    private fun mirrorNv21(nv21: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int): ByteArray {
        val mirrored = ByteArray(nv21.size)
        val ySize = width * height

        // Mirror Y plane
        for (y in 0 until height) {
            for (x in 0 until width) {
                mirrored[y * width + (width - 1 - x)] = nv21[y * width + x]
            }
        }

        // Mirror VU plane
        val chromaSize = ySize / 4
        val vuOffset = ySize
        for (i in 0 until chromaSize) {
            mirrored[vuOffset + i * 2] = nv21[vuOffset + i * 2]
            mirrored[vuOffset + i * 2 + 1] = nv21[vuOffset + i * 2 + 1]
        }

        return mirrored
    }

    /**
     * Write frame to surface.
     */
    private fun writeFrameToSurface(surface: Surface, data: ByteArray, width: Int, height: Int) {
        // Get the ANativeWindow from the Surface
        try {
            val nativeWindow = surface.surface
            if (nativeWindow != null) {
                // Lock the buffer and write data
                val buffer = nativeWindow.acquireNextBuffer(1000)
                if (buffer != null) {
                    // Copy data to the buffer
                    val ptr = buffer
                    for (i in data.indices) {
                        // Simple byte copy (in real implementation, use ByteBuffer)
                    }
                    nativeWindow.queueBuffer(buffer, -1)
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to write to surface: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Send capture result back to the client app.
     */
    private fun sendCaptureResult(
        request: android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest,
        callback: CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback?,
        handler: Handler?,
        frameSize: Int
    ) {
        if (callback == null) return

        val result = object : TotalCaptureResult(
            android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_STATE,
            android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.CONTROL_AE_STATE_CONVERGED
        ) {
            override fun get(key: android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.Key<*>): Any? {
                return when (key) {
                    android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.CONTROL_AE_STATE ->
                        android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.CONTROL_AE_STATE_CONVERGED
                    android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.CONTROL_AF_STATE ->
                        android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.CONTROL_AF_STATE_FOCUSED_POWERED
                    android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.CONTROL_AWB_STATE ->
                        android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.CONTROL_AWB_STATE_CONVERGED
                    android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.SENSOR_TIMESTAMP -> System.nanoTime()
                    android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.JPEG_ORIENTATION -> 0
                    android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult.LENS_FACING ->
                        android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_EXTERNAL
                    else -> super.get(key)
                }
            }
        }

        handler?.post {
            try {
                callback.onCaptureCompleted(this@VirtualCameraSession, request, result as TotalCaptureResult)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                logAction("$TAG: Capture callback failed: ${e.message}")
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the session is active.
     */
    fun isActive(): Boolean = isActive

    /**
     * Check if the session is paused.
     */
    fun isPaused(): Boolean = isPaused

    /**
     * Pause the session.
     */
    fun pause() {
        isPaused = true
        logAction("$TAG: Session paused")
    }

    /**
     * Resume the session.
     */
    fun resume() {
        isPaused = false
        logAction("$TAG: Session resumed")
    }

    /**
     * Stop the session.
     */
    fun stop() {
        isRepeating = false
        isPaused = true
        yuvDecoder?.stop()
        logAction("$TAG: Session stopped")
    }

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        isActive = false
        isRepeating = false
        isPaused = true
        yuvDecoder?.stop()
        captureThread.quitSafely()
        logAction("$TAG: Session released")
    }

    /**
     * Get the device ID.
     */
    fun getDeviceId(): String = deviceId

    /**
     * Get the number of output surfaces.
     */
    fun getOutputSurfaceCount(): Int = outputSurfaces.size

    /**
     * Get the output surfaces.
     */
    fun getOutputSurfaces(): List<Surface> = outputSurfaces.toList()

    /**
     * Get the stream configuration map.
     */
    fun getStreamConfigs(): android.hardware.camera2.params.StreamConfigurationMap = streamConfigs

    /**
     * Check if the session is valid.
     */
    fun isValid(): Boolean = isActive && !isPaused
}
```

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.data
object Constants {
    const val PREFS_NAME = "xcam_prefs"
    const val KEY_MEDIA_PATH = "media_path"
    const val KEY_IS_ENABLED = "is_enabled"
    const val KEY_IS_MIRRORED = "is_mirrored"
    const val KEY_ROTATION_ANGLE = "rotation_angle"
    const val AUTHORITY = "com.hazbu.xcam.provider"

    const val DEFAULT_CAPTURE_WIDTH = 1280
    const val DEFAULT_CAPTURE_HEIGHT = 1280
    const val DUMMY_SURFACE_TEXTURE_ID = 999
    const val MIN_SESSION_DEBOUNCE_MS = 100L
    const val STREAM_FRAME_INTERVAL_MS = 33L
    const val KEY_ENABLE_CAPTURE_NOTIF = "enable_capture_notif"
    const val KEY_LATEST_PIPELINE_APP = "latest_pipeline_app"
    const val KEY_LATEST_PIPELINE_ROUTE = "latest_pipeline_route"
    const val KEY_LATEST_PIPELINE_TIME = "latest_pipeline_time"

    const val METHOD_RECORD_NODE = "record_node"
    const val METHOD_NOTIFY_CAPTURE = "notify_capture"
    const val CAPTURE_NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID = "xcam_capture_channel"
    const val CAPTURE_NOTIFICATION_ID = 1001
}
```
