`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/splash/DepthIRCamera.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.spoof

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics
import android.util.Log

/**
 * Depth/IR Camera Support for KYC bypass.
 *
 * Handles depth and infrared camera channels used by premium KYC providers
 * (iProov, Onfido, Jumio, Veriff) for anti-spoofing.
 *
 * Supported camera types:
 * - DEPTH_16BIT (depth map, 16-bit per pixel)
 * - DEPTH_DEEP_LAYERS (multi-layer depth)
 * - DEPTH_DEPTH_DISParity (disparity map)
 * - DEPTH_DEPTH_DISParity_DEEP (deep disparity map)
 * - DEPTH_DEPTH_DISParity_DEEPER (deeper disparity map)
 * - DEPTH_DEPTH_DISParity_DEPTH (deeper disparity map)
 * - DEPTH_DEEP (deep depth map)
 * - DEEP (deep depth map)
 * - INVISIBLE (infrared/IR camera)
 * - IR (infrared/IR camera)
 * - IR_DEEP (deep IR camera)
 * - IR_DEEPER (deeper IR camera)
 *
 * Usage from XCamModule:
 *   val depthCam = DepthIRCamera(context)
 *   depthCam.enable()
 *   // ... app uses depth/IR camera, sees virtual frames
 *   depthCam.disable()
 */
class DepthIRCamera(
    private val context: Context?,
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xDepthIR"

        // Common depth/IR formats
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_DEPTH_16BIT = android.graphics.ImageFormat.DEPTH_DEEP_LAYERS
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_DEPTH_DEEP = android.graphics.ImageFormat.PRIVATE
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_DEPTH_DEEPER = android.graphics.ImageFormat.DEPTH_DEEPER
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_DEEP = android.graphics.ImageFormat.DEPTH_DEEP
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_IR = android.graphics.ImageFormat.DEPTH_DEEP
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_IR_DEEP = android.graphics.ImageFormat.PRIVATE
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_IR_DEEPER = android.graphics.ImageFormat.PRIVATE
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_INVISIBLE = android.graphics.ImageFormat.DEPTH_DEEP
        const val IMAGE_FORMAT_INVISIBLE_DEEP = android.graphics.ImageFormat.PRIVATE
    }

    // State
    private var isHooking = false

    // Depth map frame
    private var depthFrameWidth = 0
    private var depthFrameHeight = 0
    private var depthFrame: ByteArray? = null

    // IR frame
    private var irFrameWidth = 0
    private var irFrameHeight = 0
    private var irFrame: ByteArray? = null

    // Depth camera facing
    private var depthCameraFacing = CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_BACK
    private var irCameraFacing = CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING_BACK

    init {
        logAction("$TAG: DepthIRCamera initialized")
    }

    // ==================== ENABLE/DISABLE ====================

    fun enable() {
        if (isHooking) return
        isHooking = true
        logAction("$TAG: Depth/IR camera ENABLED")
    }

    fun disable() {
        isHooking = false
        logAction("$TAG: Depth/IR camera DISABLED")
    }

    fun isHooking(): Boolean = isHooking

    // ==================== DEPTH/MAP FRAMES ====================

    /**
     * Set a custom depth map frame.
     * @param frame YUV_420_888 or DEPTH_16BIT formatted data
     * @param width Frame width
     * @param height Frame height
     */
    fun setDepthFrame(frame: ByteArray?, width: Int, height: Int) {
        this.depthFrame = frame
        this.depthFrameWidth = width
        this.depthFrameHeight = height
        logAction("$TAG: Depth frame set: ${frame?.size ?: 0} bytes, $width x $height")
    }

    fun setDepthCameraFacing(facing: Int) {
        this.depthCameraFacing = facing
        logAction("$TAG: Depth camera facing set to: $facing")
    }

    fun setIRCameraFacing(facing: Int) {
        this.irCameraFacing = facing
        logAction("$TAG: IR camera facing set to: $facing")
    }

    // ==================== IR FRAMES ====================

    /**
     * Set a custom IR (infrared) frame.
     */
    fun setIRFrame(frame: ByteArray?, width: Int, height: Int) {
        this.irFrame = frame
        this.irFrameWidth = width
        this.irFrameHeight = height
        logAction("$TAG: IR frame set: ${frame?.size ?: 0} bytes, $width x $height")
    }

    // ==================== CAMERA CHARACTERISTICS ====================

    /**
     * Build camera characteristics for a depth/IR camera.
     */
    fun buildDepthCameraCharacteristics(cameraId: String, facing: Int): android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics {
        return android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics(cameraId).apply {
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, facing)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.HARDWARE_LEVEL, android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata.HARDWARE_LEVEL_FULL)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.INFO_SUPPORTED_HARDWARE_LEVEL, android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata.HARDWARE_LEVEL_FULL)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES, arrayOf(
                android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_PARALLEL_OUTPUT,
                android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_DEPTH_OUTPUT,
                android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_INTERNAL_DEPTH,
                android.hardware.camera2.CameraMetadata.REQUEST_AVAILABLE_CAPABILITIES_DEPTH_MAP
            ))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_ACTIVE_ARRAY_SIZE, android.graphics.Rect(0, 0, 640, 480))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_DYNAMIC_RANGE, intArrayOf(1, 2, 3, 4))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.NOISE_PROFILE, floatArrayOf(0f, 0f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.VIEW_INVERSE_SQUARE_INNER, floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.VIEW_INVERSE_SQUARE_OUTER, floatArrayOf(0f, 0f, 0f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.LENS_FACING, facing)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_FACING, facing)
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_INNER, floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_OUTER, floatArrayOf(0f, 0f, 0f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_DYNAMIC_RANGE, intArrayOf(1, 2, 3, 4))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_ACTIVE_ARRAY_SIZE, android.graphics.Rect(0, 0, 640, 480))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_NOISE_PROFILE, floatArrayOf(0f, 0f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_INVERSE_SQUARE_INNER, floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_INVERSE_SQUARE_OUTER, floatArrayOf(0f, 0f, 0f))
            set(CameraCharacteristics.STATIC_SENSOR_DYNAMIC_RANGE, intArrayOf(1, 2, 3, 4))
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get the depth camera facing.
     */
    fun getDepthCameraFacing(): Int = depthCameraFacing

    /**
     * Get the IR camera facing.
     */
    fun getIRCameraFacing(): Int = irCameraFacing

    // ==================== STATUS ====================

    /**
     * Get the current status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String {
        val state = if (isHooking) "ENABLED" else "DEPTH: $depthFrameWidth x $depthFrameHeight | IR: $irFrameWidth x $irFrameHeight"
        val cam = "DEPTH: ${getDepthCameraFacing()} | IR: ${getIRCameraFacing()}"
        return "$state | $cam"
    }

    /**
     * Check if depth/IR is ready (has frames and enabled).
     */
    fun isReady(): Boolean = isHooking && (depthFrame != null || irFrame != null)

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        disable()
        depthFrame = null
        irFrame = null
        logAction("$TAG: DepthIRCamera released")
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/splash/WebRtcDeepHooks.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.splash

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Handler
import android.util.Log
import android.view.Surface
import android.webkit.WebView
import java.nio.ByteBuffer

/**
 * WebRTC/WebView Deep Hooks for KYC bypass.
 *
 * Intercepts camera/video in WebRTC-based KYC flows:
 * - org.webrtc.Camera2Capturer (WebRTC Camera2 capturer)
 * - org.webrtc.SurfaceTextureHelper (WebRTC surface texture)
 * - org.webrtc.PeerConnection.addTrack() (fake video track injection)
 * - android.webkit.WebView.evaluateJavascript() (JS getUserMedia)
 *
 * Usage from XCamModule:
 *   val webRTC = WebRtcDeepHooks(context)
 *   webRTC.enable()
 *   // ... app uses WebRTC camera, sees virtual feed
 *   webRTC.disable()
 */
class WebRtcDeepHooks(
    private val context: Context?,
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xWebRtc"
    }

    // State
    private var isHooking = false
    private var isWebRtcAvailable = false

    // WebRTC components
    private var fakeVideoTrack: Any? = null
    private var fakeCapturer: Any? = null
    private var helperSurface: Surface? = null

    // Handler for WebRTC operations
    private val handler = Handler()

    init {
        // Check if WebRTC is available
        isWebRtcAvailable = try {
            Class.forName("org.webrtc.PeerConnection")
            true
        } catch (_: ClassNotFoundException) {
            false
        }
        logAction("$TAG: WebRTC available: $isWebRtcAvailable")
    }

    // ==================== ENABLE/DISABLE ====================

    fun enable() {
        if (isHooking) return
        isHooking = true
        logAction("$TAG: WebRTC hooks ENABLED")
    }

    fun disable() {
        isHooking = false
        logAction("$TAG: WebRTC hooks DISABLED")
    }

    fun isHooking(): Boolean = isHooking

    // ==================== WEBCRTC CAPTURER ====================

    /**
     * Create a fake WebRTC Camera2Capturer.
     * Returns null if WebRTC is not available.
     */
    fun createFakeCapturer(surface: Surface): Any? {
        if (!isWebRtcAvailable || !isHooking) return null

        try {
            val clazz = Class.forName("org.webrtc.Camera2Capturer")
            val constructor = clazz.declaredConstructors.firstOrNull { it.parameterCount == 2 }
            if (constructor != null) {
                val instance = constructor.setAccessible(true)
                return instance.newInstance(null, surface)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to create fake Camera2Capturer: ${e.message}")
        }

        return null
    }

    /**
     * Create a fake WebRTC SurfaceTextureHelper.
     */
    fun createFakeSurfaceTextureHelper(): Any? {
        if (!isWebRtcAvailable || !isHooking) return null

        try {
            val clazz = Class.forName("org.webrtc.SurfaceTextureHelper")
            val method = clazz.getDeclaredMethod("create", String::class.java)
            val instance = method.invoke(null, "xCam-WebRTC")
            return instance
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to create SurfaceTextureHelper: ${e.message}")
        }

        return null
    }

    /**
     * Hook WebRTC Camera2Capturer.start() to inject virtual camera.
     */
    fun hookCamera2CapturerStart(capturer: Any, callback: Any) {
        if (!isHooking) return

        try {
            val clazz = capturer::class.java
            val method = clazz.getDeclaredMethod("start", Int::class.java, Int::class.java)
            method.invoke(capturer, 0, 0) // Start with default resolution
            logAction("$TAG: Camera2Capturer.start() hooked")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to hook Camera2Capturer.start: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    // ==================== WEBRTC TRACKS ====================

    /**
     * Create a fake WebRTC VideoTrack.
     * Used to inject virtual camera into WebRTC peer connections.
     */
    fun createFakeVideoTrack(peerConnection: Any, trackId: String): Any? {
        if (!isWebRtcAvailable || !isHooking) return null

        try {
            val pc = peerConnection.javaClass
            val method = pc.getDeclaredMethod("createVideoTrack", String::class.java, Any::class.java)
            val track = method.invoke(peerConnection, trackId, null)
            fakeVideoTrack = track
            logAction("$TAG: Fake VideoTrack created: $trackId")
            return track
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to create VideoTrack: ${e.message}")
        }

        return null
    }

    /**
     * Add a fake video track to a WebRTC PeerConnection.
     */
    fun addFakeVideoTrack(peerConnection: Any, track: Any) {
        if (!isHooking) return

        try {
            val pc = peerConnection.javaClass
            val method = pc.getDeclaredMethod("addTrack", Any::class.java, Array<out String>::class.java)
            method.invoke(peerConnection, track, arrayOf("100"))
            logAction("$TAG: Fake video track added to PeerConnection")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to add video track: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    // ==================== WEBRTC VIEWS ====================

    /**
     * Hook WebRTC getUserMedia() in a WebView.
     * This intercepts JavaScript-level camera requests.
     */
    fun hookWebRtcGetUserMedia(webView: WebView) {
        if (!isHooking) return

        try {
            val js = """
                (function() {
                    var origGetUserMedia = navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia;
                    navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia = function(constraints) {
                        console.log('[xCam] getUserMedia called:', JSON.stringify(constraints));
                        return origGetUserMedia.call(navigator.mediaDevices, {
                            video: { width: 1280, height: 720 }
                        });
                    };
                })();
            """.trimIndent()

            webView.evaluateJavascript(js, null)
            logAction("$TAG: WebRTC getUserMedia hooked in WebView")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to hook getUserMedia: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Hook WebRTC MediaStream.addTrack() in a WebView.
     */
    fun hookWebRtcAddTrack(webView: WebView) {
        if (!isHooking) return

        try {
            val js = """
                (function() {
                    var origAddTrack = MediaStream.prototype.addTrack;
                    MediaStream.prototype.addTrack = function(track) {
                        console.log('[xCam] MediaStream.addTrack called:', track.kind);
                        return origAddTrack.call(this, track);
                    };
                })();
            """.trimIndent()

            webView.evaluateJavascript(js, null)
            logAction("$TAG: WebRTC MediaStream.addTrack hooked in WebView")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to hook MediaStream.addTrack: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Inject a fake WebRTC video track into a WebView's MediaStream.
     */
    fun injectFakeTrack(webView: WebView, track: Any, streamId: String) {
        if (!isHooking) return

        try {
            val js = """
                (function() {
                    var stream = new MediaStream();
                    var track = ${track.toString()};
                    stream.addTrack(track);
                    console.log('[xCam] Fake track injected into stream: $streamId');
                })();
            """.trimIndent()

            webView.evaluateJavascript(js, null)
            logAction("$TAG: Fake track injected into WebView: $streamId")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to inject track: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    // ==================== WEBRTC CAPTURE CALLBACKS ====================

    /**
     * Hook WebRTC Capturer callbacks.
     */
    fun hookCapturerCallbacks(capturer: Any) {
        if (!isHooking) return

        try {
            val clazz = capturer.javaClass
            val method = clazz.getDeclaredMethod("onFirstFrameAvailable", Int::class.java, Int::class.java)
            method.invoke(capturer, 1280, 720) // Report 720p as first frame
            logAction("$TAG: Capturer callbacks hooked")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to hook callbacks: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Hook WebRTC Capturer onFrameReady.
     */
    fun hookOnFrameReady(capturer: Any, buffer: ByteBuffer) {
        if (!isHooking) return

        try {
            val clazz = capturer.javaClass
            val method = clazz.getDeclaredMethod("onFrameReady", ByteBuffer::class.java, Int::class.java, Int::class.java, Long::class.java)
            method.invoke(capturer, buffer, 0, 0, System.nanoTime())
            logAction("$TAG: onFrameReady hooked")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: Failed to hook onFrameReady: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    // ==================== STATUS ====================

    /**
     * Get the current status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String {
        val state = if (isHooking) "ENABLED" else "DISABLED"
        val webRtc = if (isWebRtcAvailable) "AVAILABLE" else "NOT_AVAILABLE"
        val track = if (fakeVideoTrack != null) "TRACK_ACTIVE" else "NO_TRACK"
        return "$state | WebRTC: $webRtc | $track"
    }

    /**
     * Check if WebRTC hooks are ready.
     */
    fun isReady(): Boolean = isHooking && isWebRtcAvailable

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        disable()
        fakeVideoTrack = null
        fakeCapturer = null
        helperSurface = null
        logAction("$TAG: WebRtcDeepHooks released")
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/spoof/DeviceInfoSpoofer.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.spoof

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.os.SystemProperties
import android.telephony.TelephonyManager
import android.util.Log

/**
 * Device Info / Build Prop Spoofing.
 *
 * This module intercepts and fakes device identifiers and system properties
 * that KYC apps check for root detection, emulator detection, and device identity.
 *
 * Properties spoofed:
 * - IMEI/MEID/Device ID (TelephonyManager)
 * - Android ID (Settings.Secure.ANDROID_ID)
 * - Serial number (Build.SERIAL)
 * - Build props: HARDWARE, PRODUCT, MANUFACTURER, BRAND, MODEL, BOARD, etc.
 * - SystemProperties overrides (ro.xxx, persist.xxx)
 * - Debuggable flag (ApplicationInfo)
 * - Xposed/LSPosed detection
 *
 * Usage from XCamModule:
 *   val deviceSpoof = DeviceInfoSpoofer(context)
 *   deviceSpoof.enable()
 *   // ... target app checks device info, sees spoofed values
 *   deviceSpoof.disable()
 */
class DeviceInfoSpoofer(
    private val context: Context?,
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xDeviceSpoof"

        // Default spoofed values
        private const val DEFAULT_ANDROID_ID = "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
        private const val DEFAULT_SERIAL = "R08QB01234"
        private const val DEFAULT_IMEI = "353456789012345"
        private const val DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID = "d1e2f3a4b5c6d7e8"

        // Default Build props (pixel-like for maximum compatibility)
        private const val DEFAULT_HARDWARE = "sunfish"
        private const val DEFAULT_PRODUCT = "sdk_google_x86_64"
        private const val DEFAULT_MANUFACTURER = "Google"
        private const val DEFAULT_BRAND = "google"
        private const val DEFAULT_MODEL = "Pixel 7"
        private const val DEFAULT_BOARD = "sdk_google_x86_64"
        private const val DEFAULT_BOOTLOADER = "bootloader"
        private const val DEFAULT_RADIO = "radio"
        private const val DEFAULT_DEVICE = "sunfish"
        private const val Default_OTA = "ota"
        private const val DEFAULT_FINGERPRINT = "google/sdk_google_x86_64/sdk_google_x86_64/2026090300:15/ABC123/12345678:user/release-keys"
        private const val DEFAULT_SDK_INT = "34"
        private const val DEFAULT_RELEASE = "14"
        private const val DEFAULT_INCREMENTAL = "12345678"
        private const val DEFAULT_RELEASE_OR_VERSION = "14"
        private const val DEFAULT_CODENAME = "REL"
    }

    // State
    private var isSpoofing = false
    private var isPhone = false

    // Custom spoof values (from settings)
    private var customAndroidId: String? = null
    private var customSerial: String? = null
    private var customImei: String? = null
    private var customDeviceId: String? = null
    private var customHardware: String? = null
    private var customProduct: String? = null
    private var customManufacturer: String? = null
    private var customBrand: String? = null
    private var customModel: String? = null
    private var customBoard: String? = null
    private var customBootloader: String? = null
    private var customRadio: String? = null
    private var customDevice: String? = null
    private var customOta: String? = null
    private var customFingerprint: String? = null
    private var customSdkInt: String? = null
    private var customRelease: String? = null
    private var customIncremental: String? = null
    private var customCodename: String? = null

    // Original values (for restoring)
    private var originalAndroidId: String = DEFAULT_ANDROID_ID
    private var originalSerial: String = DEFAULT_SERIAL
    private var originalImei: String = DEFAULT_IMEI
    private var originalDeviceId: String = DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID
    private var originalHardware: String = DEFAULT_HARDWARE
    private var originalProduct: String = DEFAULT_PRODUCT
    private var originalManufacturer: String = DEFAULT_MANUFACTURER
    private var originalBrand: String = DEFAULT_BRAND
    private var originalModel: String = DEFAULT_MODEL
    private var originalBoard: String = DEFAULT_BOARD
    private var originalBootloader: String = DEFAULT_BOOTLOADER
    private var originalRadio: String = DEFAULT_RADIO
    private var originalDevice: String = DEFAULT_DEVICE
    private var originalOta: String = DEFAULT_OTA
    private var originalFingerprint: String = DEFAULT_FINGERPRINT

    // System properties to override
    private val systemProps = mutableMapOf<String, String>()

    init {
        // Read original values
        originalAndroidId = getOriginalAndroidId()
        originalSerial = getOriginalSerial()
        originalImei = getOriginalImei()
        originalDeviceId = getOriginalDeviceId()
        originalHardware = getOriginalHardware()
        originalProduct = getOriginalProduct()
        originalManufacturer = getOriginalManufacturer()
        originalBrand = getOriginalBrand()
        originalModel = getOriginalModel()
        originalBoard = getOriginalBoard()
        originalBootloader = getOriginalBootloader()
        originalRadio = getOriginalRadio()
        originalDevice = getOriginalDevice()
        originalOta = getOriginalOta()
        originalFingerprint = getOriginalFingerprint()
    }

    /**
     * Enable device info spoofing.
     */
    fun enable() {
        if (isSpoofing) {
            logAction("$TAG: Already enabled")
            return
        }

        isSpoofing = true
        logAction("$TAG: Device info spoofing ENABLED")
    }

    /**
     * Disable device info spoofing.
     */
    fun disable() {
        if (!isSpoofing) {
            logAction("$TAG: Already disabled")
            return
        }

        isSpoofing = false
        logAction("$TAG: Device info spoofing DISABLED")
    }

    /**
     * Check if spoofing is active.
     */
    fun isSpoofing(): Boolean = isSpoofing

    /**
     * Enable phone mode (for devices with telephony).
     */
    fun enablePhoneMode() {
        isPhone = true
        logAction("$TAG: Phone mode ENABLED")
    }

    /**
     * Disable phone mode.
     */
    fun disablePhoneMode() {
        isPhone = false
        logAction("$TAG: Phone mode DISABLED")
    }

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Android ID.
     */
    fun getAndroidId(): String = customAndroidId ?: originalAndroidId

    /**
     * Get the spoofed IMEI.
     */
    fun getImei(): String = if (isPhone) {
        customImei ?: originalImei
    } else {
        "000000000000000"
    }

    /**
     * Get the spoofed device ID.
     */
    fun getDeviceId(): String = if (isPhone) {
        customDeviceId ?: originalDeviceId
    } else {
        "0000000000000000"
    }

    /**
     * Get the spoofed serial number.
     */
    fun getSerial(): String = customSerial ?: originalSerial

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.HARDWARE.
     */
    fun getHardware(): String = customHardware ?: originalHardware

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.PRODUCT.
     */
    fun getProduct(): String = customProduct ?: originalProduct

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.MANUFACTURER.
     */
    fun getManufacturer(): String = customManufacturer ?: originalManufacturer

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.BRAND.
     */
    fun getBrand(): String = customBrand ?: originalBrand

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.MODEL.
     */
    fun getModel(): String = customModel ?: originalModel

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.BOARD.
     */
    fun getBoard(): String = customBoard ?: originalBoard

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.BOOTSLOADER.
     */
    fun getBootloader(): String = customBootloader ?: originalBootloader

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.RADIO.
     */
    fun getRadio(): String = customRadio ?: originalRadio

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.DEVICE.
     */
    fun getDevice(): String = customDevice ?: originalDevice

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.OTA.
     */
    fun getOta(): String = customOta ?: originalOta

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.FINGERPRINT.
     */
    fun getFingerprint(): String = customFingerprint ?: originalFingerprint

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.VERSION.SDK_INT.
     */
    fun getSdkInt(): Int = (customSdkInt ?: originalSdkInt)?.toInt() ?: Build.VERSION.SDK_INT

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.VERSION.RELEASE.
     */
    fun getRelease(): String = customRelease ?: Build.VERSION.RELEASE

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.VERSION.INCREMENTAL.
     */
    fun getIncremental(): String = customIncremental ?: Build.VERSION.INCREMENTAL

    /**
     * Get the spoofed Build.VERSION.CODENAME.
     */
    fun getCodename(): String = customCodename ?: Build.VERSION.CODENAME

    /**
     * Set a custom Android ID.
     */
    fun setAndroidId(id: String?) {
        this.customAndroidId = id
        logAction("$TAG: Android ID set to: $id")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom IMEI.
     */
    fun setImei(imei: String?) {
        this.customImei = imei
        logAction("$TAG: IMEI set to: $imei")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom device ID.
     */
    fun setDeviceId(deviceId: String?) {
        this.customDeviceId = deviceId
        logAction("$TAG: Device ID set to: $deviceId")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom serial number.
     */
    fun setSerial(serial: String?) {
        this.customSerial = serial
        logAction("$TAG: Serial set to: $serial")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom hardware name.
     */
    fun setHardware(hardware: String?) {
        this.customHardware = hardware
        logAction("$TAG: Hardware set to: $hardware")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom product name.
     */
    fun setProduct(product: String?) {
        this.customProduct = product
        logAction("$TAG: Product set to: $product")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom manufacturer.
     */
    fun setManufacturer(manufacturer: String?) {
        this.customManufacturer = manufacturer
        logAction("$TAG: Manufacturer set to: $manufacturer")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom brand.
     */
    fun setBrand(brand: String?) {
        this.customBrand = brand
        logAction("$TAG: Brand set to: $brand")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom model.
     */
    fun setModel(model: String?) {
        this.customModel = model
        logAction("$TAG: Model set to: $model")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom board.
     */
    fun setBoard(board: String?) {
        this.customBoard = board
        logAction("$TAG: Board set to: $board")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom bootloader.
     */
    fun setBootloader(bootloader: String?) {
        this.customBootloader = bootloader
        logAction("$TAG: Bootloader set to: $bootloader")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom radio version.
     */
    fun setRadio(radio: String?) {
        this.customRadio = radio
        logAction("$TAG: Radio set to: $radio")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom device name.
     */
    fun setDevice(device: String?) {
        this.customDevice = device
        logAction("$TAG: Device set to: $device")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom OTA version.
     */
    fun setOta(ota: String?) {
        this.customOta = ota
        logAction("$TAG: OTA set to: $ota")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom fingerprint.
     */
    fun setFingerprint(fingerprint: String?) {
        this.customFingerprint = fingerprint
        logAction("$TAG: Fingerprint set to: $fingerprint")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom SDK_INT.
     */
    fun setSdkInt(sdkInt: String?) {
        this.customSdkInt = sdkInt
        logAction("$TAG: SDK_INT set to: $sdkInt")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom VERSION.RELEASE.
     */
    fun setRelease(release: String?) {
        this.customRelease = release
        logAction("$TAG: RELEASE set to: $release")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom VERSION.INCREMENTAL.
     */
    fun setIncremental(incremental: String?) {
        this.customIncremental = incremental
        logAction("$TAG: INCREMENTAL set to: $incremental")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom VERSION.CODENAME.
     */
    fun setCodename(codename: String?) {
        this.customCodename = codename
        logAction("$TAG: CODENAME set to: $codename")
    }

    /**
     * Set a custom system property override.
     *
     * Format: "ro.property.name" = "value"
     *
     * These will intercept calls to:
     * - android.os.SystemProperties.get("ro.property.name")
     * - android.os.SystemProperties.get("ro.property.name", "default")
     * - SystemProperties.getBoolean("ro.property.name", false)
     * - SystemProperties.getLong("ro.property.name", 0L)
     * - SystemProperties.getInt("ro.property.name", 0)
     */
    fun setSystemProperty(key: String, value: String) {
        systemProps[key] = value
        logAction("$TAG: System property set: $key = $value")
    }

    /**
     * Remove a custom system property override.
     */
    fun removeSystemProperty(key: String) {
        systemProps.remove(key)
        logAction("$TAG: System property removed: $key")
    }

    /**
     * Get all custom system property overrides.
     */
    fun getSystemProperties(): Map<String, String> = systemProps.toMap()

    /**
     * Clear all custom system property overrides.
     */
    fun clearSystemProperties() {
        systemProps.clear()
        logAction("$TAG: All system property overrides cleared")
    }

    /**
     * Check if a system property has a custom override.
     */
    fun hasSystemPropertyOverride(key: String): Boolean = systemProps.containsKey(key)

    /**
     * Get the current spoof status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String {
        val state = when {
            !isSpoofing -> "DISABLED"
            isPhone -> "ENABLED (phone mode)"
            else -> "ENABLED"
        }
        val props = systemProps.size
        return "$state (${props} system property overrides)"
    }

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        disable()
        clearSystemProperties()
        logAction("$TAG: Device info spoofer released")
    }

    // Helper methods to get original values
    private fun getOriginalAndroidId(): String = try {
        android.provider.Settings.Secure.getString(
            context?.contentResolver,
            android.provider.Settings.Secure.ANDROID_ID
        ) ?: DEFAULT_ANDROID_ID
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        DEFAULT_ANDROID_ID
    }

    private fun getOriginalSerial(): String = try {
        Build.SERIAL ?: DEFAULT_SERIAL
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        DEFAULT_SERIAL
    }

    private fun getOriginalImei(): String = try {
        val tm = context?.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE) as? TelephonyManager
        tm?.ime
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        DEFAULT_IMEI
    }

    private fun getOriginalDeviceId(): String = try {
        val tm = context?.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE) as? TelephonyManager
        tm?.deviceId
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        DEFAULT_DEVICE_ID
    }

    private fun getOriginalHardware(): String = Build.HARDWARE
    private fun getOriginalProduct(): String = Build.PRODUCT
    private fun getOriginalManufacturer(): String = Build.MANUFACTURER
    private fun getOriginalBrand(): String = Build.BRAND
    private fun getOriginalModel(): String = Build.MODEL
    private fun getOriginalBoard(): String = Build.BOARD
    private fun getOriginalBootloader(): String = Build.BOOTSLOADER
    private fun getOriginalRadio(): String = Build.RADIO
    private fun getOriginalDevice(): String = Build.DEVICE
    private fun getOriginalOta(): String = Build.OTA
    private fun getOriginalFingerprint(): String = Build.FINGERPRINT
    private fun getOriginalSdkInt(): String = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT.toString()

    /**
     * Get a system property value (with spoofing).
     *
     * This is called by the Xposed hook that intercepts
     * android.os.SystemProperties.get(key, defaultValue).
     */
    fun getSystemPropertyValue(key: String, defaultValue: String): String {
        if (!isSpoofing) return defaultValue

        val override = systemProps[key]
        if (override != null) {
            return override
        }

        // Default overrides for common KYC checks
        return when (key) {
            "ro.build.selinux" -> "1" // SELinux is enabled
            "ro.debuggable" -> "0" // Not debuggable
            "ro.secure" -> "1" // Secure
            "ro.hardware" -> getHardware()
            "ro.product.board" -> getBoard()
            "ro.product.brand" -> getBrand()
            "ro.product.device" -> getDevice()
            "ro.product.manufacturer" -> getManufacturer()
            "ro.product.model" -> getModel()
            "ro.product.name" -> getProduct()
            "ro.bootloader" -> getBootloader()
            "ro.boot.hardware" -> getHardware()
            "ro.boot.ram" -> getRadio()
            "ro.boot.ota" -> getOta()
            "ro.boot.fingerprint" -> getFingerprint()
            "ro.build.display.id" -> getFingerprint()
            "ro.build.version.release" -> getRelease()
            "ro.build.version.sdk" -> getSdkInt().toString()
            "ro.build.version.incremental" -> getIncremental()
            "ro.build.version.codename" -> getCodename()
            "ro.build.type" -> "user"
            "ro.build.tags" -> "release-keys"
            "ro.build.description" -> getProduct()
            "ro.build.characteristics" -> "nosdcard"
            "persist.sys.dalvik.vm.lib.2" -> "libart.so"
            "dalvik.vm.heapsize" -> "512m"
            "ro.config.ringtone" -> "Ring_Synth_04.ogg"
            "ro.com.google.clientid" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.gmsversion" -> getRelease()
            "ro.com.google.appbrand" -> "google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.analytics" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.gf" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.gmp" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.gmm" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.ya" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.ya" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.ya" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.ya" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.ya" -> "android-google"
            else -> defaultValue
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get a system property value (with spoofing and no default).
     *
     * This is called by the Xposed hook that intercepts
     * android.os.SystemProperties.get(key).
     */
    fun getSystemPropertyValue(key: String): String {
        return getSystemPropertyValue(key, "")
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/spoof/FingerprintSpoof.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.spoof

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.biometrics.BiometricPrompt
import android.os.Build
import android.os.CancellationSignal
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.util.Log

/**
 * Fingerprint Sensor Spoofing for non-rooted devices.
 *
 * Intercepts fingerprint authentication at the BiometricPrompt/FingerprintManager level
 * and returns success with fake/recorded biometric data.
 *
 * Supported APIs:
 * - FingerprintManager.authenticate() (pre-Android 10)
 * - BiometricPrompt.authenticate() (Android 10+)
 * - FingerprintSensor.onFrame() (raw fingerprint image)
 * - IInstanceManager.getFingerprint() (system-level fingerprint service)
 *
 * Usage from XCamModule:
 *   val fpSpoof = FingerprintSpoof(context)
 *   fpSpoof.enable()
 *   fpSpoof.setRecordedFingerprint(imageBytes) // optional
 *   // ... target app authenticates, sees success
 *   fpSpoof.disable()
 */
class FingerprintSpoof(
    private val context: Context?,
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xFpSpoof"
    }

    // State
    private var isSpoofing = false
    private var isPhone = false

    // Record fingerprint image (optional - if set, returns this image)
    private var recordedFingerprint: ByteArray? = null

    // Authentication state
    @Volatile
    private var lastAuthResult: Boolean = false
    @Volatile
    private var lastAuthTime: Long = 0L

    // Handler for async callbacks
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    // Listener for authentication events
    private var authListener: FingerprintAuthListener? = null

    init {
        logAction("$TAG: FingerprintSpoof initialized")
    }

    // ==================== ENABLE/DISABLE ====================

    fun enable() {
        if (isSpoofing) return
        isSpoofing = true
        logAction("$TAG: Fingerprint spoofing ENABLED")
    }

    fun disable() {
        isSpoofing = false
        logAction("$TAG: Fingerprint spoofing DISABLED")
    }

    fun isSpoofing(): Boolean = isSpoofing

    fun enablePhoneMode() { isPhone = true; logAction("$TAG: Phone mode enabled") }
    fun disablePhoneMode() { isPhone = false; logAction("$TAG: Phone mode disabled") }
    fun isPhoneMode(): Boolean = isPhone

    // ==================== RECORDING ====================

    fun setRecordedFingerprint(imageBytes: ByteArray?) {
        this.recordedFingerprint = imageBytes
        logAction("$TAG: Recorded fingerprint set (${imageBytes?.size ?: 0} bytes)")
    }

    fun getRecordedFingerprint(): ByteArray? = recordedFingerprint

    fun clearRecordedFingerprint() {
        recordedFingerprint = null
        logAction("$TAG: Recorded fingerprint cleared")
    }

    // ==================== AUTHENTICATION ====================

    /**
     * Authenticate with the spoofed fingerprint.
     * This is called by the hook when the target app requests fingerprint auth.
     */
    fun authenticate(): Boolean {
        if (!isSpoofing) return false

        lastAuthResult = true
        lastAuthTime = System.currentTimeMillis()

        // Notify listener if set
        authListener?.onAuthSuccess()

        logAction("$TAG: Auth SUCCESS (spoofed)")
        return true
    }

    /**
     * Authenticate with a specific result.
     * Use this to simulate failed auth.
     */
    fun authenticate(success: Boolean) {
        lastAuthResult = success
        lastAuthTime = System.currentTimeMillis()

        if (success) {
            authListener?.onAuthSuccess()
            logAction("$TAG: Auth SUCCESS (spoofed)")
        } else {
            authListener?.onAuthFailed()
            logAction("$TAG: Auth FAILED (spoofed)")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the last authentication was successful.
     */
    fun getLastAuthResult(): Boolean = lastAuthResult

    /**
     * Get the timestamp of the last authentication.
     */
    fun getLastAuthTime(): Long = lastAuthTime

    /**
     * Check if authentication is allowed (spoofing is enabled and phone mode active).
     */
    fun isAuthAllowed(): Boolean = isSpoofing && isPhone

    // ==================== LISTENER ====================

    /**
     * Set a listener for authentication events.
     */
    fun setAuthListener(listener: FingerprintAuthListener?) {
        this.authListener = listener
    }

    /**
     * Interface for fingerprint authentication events.
     */
    interface FingerprintAuthListener {
        fun onAuthSuccess()
        fun onAuthFailed()
        fun onAuthError(message: String)
    }

    // ==================== RAW FRAGMENT (Android 11+) ====================

    /**
     * Get a fake fingerprint sensor frame (Android 11+).
     * Returns null if no recorded fingerprint is set.
     */
    fun getFakeSensorFrame(): ByteArray? {
        if (!isSpoofing) return null
        return recordedFingerprint
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device has a fingerprint sensor.
     */
    fun hasFingerprintSensor(): Boolean {
        return try {
            val pm = context?.packageManager
            pm?.hasSystemFeature(android.content.pm.PackageManager.FEATURE_FINGERPRINT) ?: false
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    // ==================== STATUS ====================

    /**
     * Get the current spoof status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String {
        val state = if (isSpoofing) "ENABLED" else "DISABLED"
        val phone = if (isPhone) "PHONE" else "NO_PHONE"
        val auth = if (lastAuthResult) "LAST_AUTH: SUCCESS" else "LAST_AUTH: FAILED"
        val fp = if (recordedFingerprint != null) "RECORDED_FP: YES" else "RECORDED_FP: NO"
        return "$state | $phone | $auth | $fp"
    }

    /**
     * Check if the fingerprint spoof is ready (enabled, phone mode, and optionally has recorded FP).
     */
    fun isReady(): Boolean = isSpoofing && isPhone

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        disable()
        clearRecordedFingerprint()
        authListener = null
        logAction("$TAG: FingerprintSpoof released")
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/spoof/RootDetectionBypass.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.spoof

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.SystemProperties
import android.telephony.TelephonyManager
import android.util.Log

/**
 * Root / Xposed / Emulator Detection Bypass.
 *
 * Intercepts and fakes the common checks that KYC apps use to detect:
 * - Root access (su binary, Magisk, SuperSU, etc.)
 * - Xposed/LSPosed framework (XposedBridge.getXposedVersion())
 * - Emulator/simulated device (TelephonyManager, Build props)
 * - Debuggable flag (ApplicationInfo)
 * - SELinux status
 * - System property checks (ro.debuggable, ro.secure, etc.)
 *
 * Usage from XCamModule:
 *   val rootBypass = RootDetectionBypass(context)
 *   rootBypass.enable()
 *   // ... target app checks for root, sees clean state
 *   rootBypass.disable()
 */
class RootDetectionBypass(
    private val context: Context?,
    private val logAction: (String) -> Unit
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "xRootBypass"
    }

    // State
    private var isBypassing = false

    // Custom override values (null = use original)
    private var customSuExists: Boolean? = null
    private var customMagiskExists: Boolean? = null
    private var customXposedVersion: String? = null
    private var customDebuggable: Int? = null
    private var customSelinuxEnabled: Boolean? = null
    private var customIsEmulator: Boolean? = null

    // Root app packages that indicate root is installed
    private val rootAppPackages = arrayOf(
        "com.noshufou.android.su",
        "com.noshufou.android.su.daemon",
        "com.jrummy.root.app.binder",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootbrowser",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootexplorer",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootchecker",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootcontrol",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootfix",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootmenu",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootnav",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootpad",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootrec",
        "com.jrummy.apps.rootrover",
        "com.jrummy.apps.shelterm",
        "com.jrummy.apps.termux",
        "com.topjohnwu.magisk",
        "com.topjohnwu.magisk.bootcomplete",
        "com.topjohnwu.magisk.repo",
        "eu.chainfire.supersu",
        "com.koushikdutta.supersu",
        "com.dimonluke.supersu",
        "com.thirdparty.supersu.thirdparty",
        "com.arvin.pandora",
        "com.devadvance.rootcloak",
        "com.devadvance.rootcloakplus",
        "com.koushikdutta.rominfo",
        "com.koushikdutta.superuser",
        "me.browniebox.superuser",
        "com.zachspong.tempest",
        "com.mgeeky.petitpower",
        "com.stericson.rootutils",
        "com.jack.vpn",
    )

    // System properties that need interception
    private val systemProps = mutableMapOf<String, String>()

    init {
        logAction("$TAG: RootDetectionBypass initialized")
    }

    // ==================== ENABLE/DISABLE ====================

    fun enable() {
        if (isBypassing) return
        isBypassing = true
        logAction("$TAG: Root detection bypass ENABLED")
    }

    fun disable() {
        isBypassing = false
        logAction("$TAG: Root detection bypass DISABLED")
    }

    fun isBypassing(): Boolean = isBypassing

    // ==================== CUSTOM OVERRIDES ====================

    fun setSuExists(suExists: Boolean?) {
        this.customSuExists = suExists
        logAction("$TAG: su_exists overridden to: $suExists")
    }

    fun getSuExists(): Boolean = customSuExists ?: checkSuExists()

    fun setMagiskExists(magiskExists: Boolean?) {
        this.customMagiskExists = magiskExists
        logAction("$TAG: magisk_exists overridden to: $magiskExists")
    }

    fun getMagiskExists(): Boolean = customMagiskExists ?: checkMagiskExists()

    fun setXposedVersion(xposedVersion: String?) {
        this.customXposedVersion = xposedVersion
        logAction("$TAG: xposed_version overridden to: $xposedVersion")
    }

    fun getXposedVersion(): String = customXposedVersion ?: checkXposedVersion()

    fun setDebuggable(debuggable: Int?) {
        this.customDebuggable = debuggable
        logAction("$TAG: debuggable overridden to: $debuggable")
    }

    fun getDebuggable(): Int = customDebuggable ?: checkDebuggable()

    fun setSelinuxEnabled(selinuxEnabled: Boolean?) {
        this.customSelinuxEnabled = selinuxEnabled
        logAction("$TAG: selinux_enabled overridden to: $selinuxEnabled")
    }

    fun getSelinuxEnabled(): Boolean = customSelinuxEnabled ?: checkSelinuxEnabled()

    fun setIsEmulator(isEmulator: Boolean?) {
        this.customIsEmulator = isEmulator
        logAction("$TAG: is_emulator overridden to: $isEmulator")
    }

    fun getIsEmulator(): Boolean = customIsEmulator ?: checkIsEmulator()

    // ==================== SYSTEM PROPERTIES ====================

    fun setSystemProperty(key: String, value: String) {
        systemProps[key] = value
        logAction("$TAG: System property set: $key = $value")
    }

    fun removeSystemProperty(key: String) {
        systemProps.remove(key)
    }

    fun getSystemProperties(): Map<String, String> = systemProps.toMap()

    fun clearSystemProperties() {
        systemProps.clear()
    }

    /**
     * Get a system property value with root bypass overrides.
     */
    fun getSystemPropertyValue(key: String, defaultValue: String): String {
        if (!isBypassing) return defaultValue

        // Check custom overrides first
        systemProps[key]?.let { return it }

        // Apply default bypass overrides
        return when (key) {
            "ro.debuggable" -> "0".also { logAction("$TAG: Intercepted ro.debuggable -> 0") }
            "ro.secure" -> "1"
            "ro.build.selinux" -> "1"
            "persist.sys.dalvik.vm.lib.2" -> "libart.so"
            "dalvik.vm.heapsize" -> "512m"
            "ro.config.ringtone" -> "Ring_Synth_04.ogg"
            "ro.com.google.clientid" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.ya" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.gm" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.gmm" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.gmp" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.analytics" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.gf" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.iam" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.om" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.oml" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.pcm" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.wifi" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.wifi" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.gps" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.bluetooth" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.nfc" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.telephony" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.sms" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.mms" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.calls" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.voicemail" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.calendar" -> "android-google"

            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.playstationvita" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.playstationvita" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.playstationvita" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.playstationvita" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.playstationvita" -> "android-google"
            "ro.com.google.clientidbase.android.playstationvita" -> "android-google"
            else -> defaultValue
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get a system property value with no default.
     */
    fun getSystemPropertyValue(key: String): String {
        return getSystemPropertyValue(key, "")
    }

    // ==================== CHECKS ====================

    /**
     * Check if su binary exists.
     */
    private fun checkSuExists(): Boolean {
        val paths = arrayOf(
            "/system/bin/su",
            "/system/xbin/su",
            "/system/sbin/su",
            "/sbin/su",
            "/vendor/bin/su",
            "/data/local/tmp/su",
            "/data/local/su",
            "/dev/.hidl-bypass/su",
            "/cache/su",
            "/cust/bin/su",
            "/data/adb/su",
            "/data/adb/ksu/bin/su",
            "/data/adb/ap/bin/su",
            "/firmware/su",
            "/system/priv-app/Superuser/bin/su",
            "/system/priv-app/Superuser/su",
            "/system/app/Superuser/bin/su",
            "/system/app/Superuser/su",
            "/system/app/Superuser/Superuser.apk",
            "/system/app/Kingroot/kernelsu",
            "/system/app/Kinguser/kernelsu",
            "/system/bin/failsafe/su",
            "/system/bin/cache/su",
            "/system/bin/cache/eu.chainfire.supersu",
            "/system/bin/cache/eu.chainfire.supersu.bin",
            "/system/bin/cache/eu.chainfire.supersu.png",
        )
        for (path in paths) {
            try {
                val f = java.io.File(path)
                if (f.exists() && f.canExecute()) {
                    logAction("$TAG: Found su at: $path")
                    return true
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
        return false
    }

    /**
     * Check if Magisk is installed.
     */
    private fun checkMagiskExists(): Boolean {
        val magiskPaths = arrayOf(
            "/sbin/.magisk",
            "/sbin/.magisk.db",
            "/sbin/.magisk.conf",
            "/sbin/.magisk.mod",
            "/sbin/.magisk.apk",
            "/sbin/.magisk.mnt",
            "/sbin/.magisk.lock",
            "/sbin/.magisk.uninstall",
            "/sbin/.magisk.version",
            "/sbin/.magisk/blank.png",
            "/sbin/.magisk/relink",
            "/sbin/.magisk/check.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/init.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/install",
            "/sbin/.magisk/post-fs-data.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/service.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/verify.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/uninstall.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/updater",
            "/sbin/.magisk/updater.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.db",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.conf",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.mod",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.apk",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.mnt",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.lock",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.uninstall",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.version",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/blank.png",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/relink",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/check.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/init.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/install",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/post-fs-data.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/service.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/verify.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/uninstall.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/updater",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk/updater.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/updater",
            "/sbin/.magisk/updater.sh",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.db",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.conf",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.mod",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.apk",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.mnt",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.lock",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.uninstall",
            "/sbin/.magisk/magisk.version",
        )
        for (path in magiskPaths) {
            try {
                val f = java.io.File(path)
                if (f.exists()) return true
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
        // Also check via package manager
        try {
            val pm = context?.packageManager
            if (pm != null) {
                val packages = pm.getInstalledPackages(PackageManager.GET_ACTIVITIES).map { it.packageName }
                if (packages.contains("com.topjohnwu.magisk")) return true
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        return false
    }

    /**
     * Check Xposed version via XposedBridge.
     */
    private fun checkXposedVersion(): String {
        try {
            val clazz = Class.forName("de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge")
            val method = clazz.getDeclaredMethod("getXposedVersion")
            val version = method.invoke(null) as String
            logAction("$TAG: Found Xposed version: $version")
            return version
        } catch (_: ClassNotFoundException) {
            // LSPosed
            try {
                val clazz = Class.forName("io.github.lsposed.binding.XposedBridge")
                val method = clazz.getDeclaredMethod("getXposedVersion")
                val version = method.invoke(null) as String
                logAction("$TAG: Found LSPosed version: $version")
                return version
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // No Xposed found
                logAction("$TAG: No Xposed framework found")
                return "0.0"
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            logAction("$TAG: XposedBridge check failed")
            return "0.0"
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check debuggable flag.
     */
    private fun checkDebuggable(): Int {
        return try {
            val pm = context?.packageManager
            val ai = pm?.getApplicationInfo(context?.packageName ?: "", 0)
            (ai?.flags?.and(android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE) ?: 0).toInt()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            0
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check SELinux enabled status.
     */
    private fun checkSelinuxEnabled(): Boolean {
        return try {
            val value = SystemProperties.get("ro.build.selinux", "1")
            value != "0"
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            true
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if device is an emulator.
     */
    private fun checkIsEmulator(): Boolean {
        // Check Build.PRODUCT for common emulator values
        val emulatorProducts = arrayOf(
            "sdk",
            "sdk_x86",
            "google_sdk",
            "droidhat",
            "emulator",
            "sdk_x86",
            "goldfish",
            "vbox86p"
        )
        if (emulatorProducts.any { Build.PRODUCT.contains(it, ignoreCase = true) }) {
            logAction("$TAG: Emulator detected via Build.PRODUCT: ${Build.PRODUCT}")
            return true
        }

        // Check Build.MODEL for emulator indicators
        if (Build.MODEL.contains("emulator", ignoreCase = true) ||
            Build.MODEL.contains("vbox86p", ignoreCase = true) ||
            Build.MODEL.contains("sdk", ignoreCase = true) ||
            Build.MODEL.contains("rover", ignoreCase = true)) {
            return true
        }

        // Check Build.HARDWARE
        if (Build.HARDWARE in arrayOf("goldfish", "vbox86", "ranchu")) {
            logAction("$TAG: Emulator detected via Build.HARDWARE: ${Build.HARDWARE}")
            return true
        }

        // Check TelephonyManager for emulator (no SIM, no IMEI)
        try {
            val tm = context?.getSystemService(Context.TELEPHONY_SERVICE) as? TelephonyManager
            if (tm != null) {
                val networkOperator = tm.networkOperatorName
                if (networkOperator == "android" || networkOperator == "emulator" || networkOperator == "vbox86p" ||
                    networkOperator == "android.sim" || networkOperator == "android.emu" ||
                    networkOperator == "android.emu.sim" || networkOperator == "android.emu.nosim" ||
                    networkOperator == "android.emu.roaming" || networkOperator == "android.emu.roaming.sim" ||
                    networkOperator == "android.emu.roaming.nosim" || networkOperator == "android.emu.roaming.nosim") {
                    logAction("$TAG: Emulator detected via TelephonyManager: $networkOperator")
                    return true
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}

        // Check /dev/socket/properties
        try {
            val propsFile = java.io.File("/dev/socket/properties")
            if (propsFile.exists()) return true
        } catch (_: Exception) {}

        return false
    }

    /**
     * Check for root app packages.
     */
    fun checkForRootApps(): Boolean {
        if (!isBypassing) return false

        val pm = context?.packageManager ?: return false

        for (pkg in rootAppPackages) {
            try {
                if (pm.getPackageInfo(pkg, 0) != null) {
                    logAction("$TAG: Root app found: $pkg")
                    return true
                }
            } catch (_: PackageManager.NameNotFoundException) {}
        }
        return false
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device has root access.
     */
    fun hasRootAccess(): Boolean {
        if (!isBypassing) return checkSuExists()

        // If su doesn't exist, no root
        if (!getSuExists()) return false

        // Check for root apps
        if (checkForRootApps()) return true

        // Check via su command
        try {
            val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec(arrayOf("su", "-c", "id"))
            process.waitFor()
            val output = process.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText()
            return output.contains("uid=0") || output.contains("root")
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            return false
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if the device is an emulator.
     */
    fun isEmulator(): Boolean = getIsEmulator()

    /**
     * Get the current bypass status.
     */
    fun getStatus(): String {
        val state = if (isBypassing) "ENABLED" else "DISABLED"
        val rootCheck = if (hasRootAccess()) "ROOT_DETECTED" else "CLEAN"
        val emuCheck = if (isEmulator()) "EMULATOR_DETECTED" else "PHYSICAL"
        return "$state | Root: $rootCheck | Emulator: $emuCheck"
    }

    /**
     * Release all resources.
     */
    fun release() {
        disable()
        clearSystemProperties()
        logAction("$TAG: RootDetectionBypass released")
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/com/hazbu/xcam/ui/CaptureNotificationHelper.kt`:

```kt
package com.hazbu.xcam.ui

import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.hazbu.xcam.R
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants.CAPTURE_NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID
import com.hazbu.xcam.data.Constants.CAPTURE_NOTIFICATION_ID

object CaptureNotificationHelper {

    fun showCaptureNotification(
        context: Context,
        packageName: String,
        route: String,
        width: Int,
        height: Int,
        thumbnailBytes: ByteArray?
    ) {
        val notificationManager = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as? NotificationManager
            ?: return

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                CAPTURE_NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID,
                "xCam Capture Alerts",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_HIGH
            ).apply {
                description = "Heads-up alert when virtual photo is injected"
                enableVibration(true)
                vibrationPattern = longArrayOf(0, 150, 100, 150)
            }
            notificationManager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }

        val pm = context.packageManager
        val appName = try {
            val appInfo = pm.getApplicationInfo(packageName, 0)
            pm.getApplicationLabel(appInfo).toString()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            packageName.substringAfterLast('.')
        }

        val openIntent = Intent(context, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
        }
        val openPendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            context,
            0,
            openIntent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val thumbnailBitmap = if (thumbnailBytes != null && thumbnailBytes.isNotEmpty()) {
            try {
                BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(thumbnailBytes, 0, thumbnailBytes.size)
            } catch (_: Throwable) {
                null
            }
        } else null

        val resolutionText = if (width > 0 && height > 0) "${width}x${height}" else "Auto"
        val bigSummary = "App: $appName ($packageName)\nPipeline: $route\nSize: $resolutionText"

        val builder = NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CAPTURE_NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL_ID)
            .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic_launcher)
            .setContentTitle("xCam: $appName")
            .setContentText("Pipeline: $route")
            .setStyle(NotificationCompat.BigTextStyle().bigText(bigSummary))
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_HIGH)
            .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_EVENT)
            .setDefaults(NotificationCompat.DEFAULT_ALL)
            .setAutoCancel(true)
            .setContentIntent(openPendingIntent)

        if (thumbnailBitmap != null) {
            builder.setLargeIcon(thumbnailBitmap)
        }

        notificationManager.notify(CAPTURE_NOTIFICATION_ID, builder.build())
    }
}
```
